===== OUTPUT_generate_use_case OUTPUT =====
Use Case: Manage E-Shop Backend System
Primary Actor: User (Administrator/Customer)
Goal: Manage users, products, carts, reservations, and orders in an e-shop backend system while ensuring correct handling of current available stock.

System Responsibilities:
- Manage user accounts, including creation and deletion.
- Maintain product catalog with attributes (name, price, stock).
- Handle cart operations (add/remove products) and reservations.
- Process checkout to create orders with atomicity.
- Update order statuses following strict transition rules.
- Ensure stock consistency during reservations, checkouts, and cancellations.
- Generate globally unique and sequential order IDs.
- Provide access to current system state (users, products, stock, carts, orders).

Preconditions:
- The e-shop backend system is initialized and operational.
- No prior state corruption exists (e.g., stock inconsistencies).

Postconditions:
- System state reflects all successful operations (user/product management, cart modifications, checkouts, status updates).
- Stock levels, reservations, and orders remain consistent.
- Order IDs are generated sequentially and uniquely.

---

**Use Case: Create User**
Primary Actor: Administrator
Goal: Add a new user to the system with a unique ID and an empty cart.

System Responsibilities:
- Validate uniqueness of user ID.
- Create an empty cart for the new user.

Preconditions:
- System is operational.

Postconditions:
- New user is added to the system.
- User has an empty cart.

Main Success Scenario:
1. Administrator requests to create a user with a specified ID.
2. System validates that the ID is unique.
3. System creates the user with the given ID.
4. System initializes an empty cart for the user.

Extensions:
2a. User ID already exists → System ignores the request.

---

**Use Case: Delete User**
Primary Actor: Administrator
Goal: Remove a user from the system, including their cart and reservations, while maintaining stock consistency.

System Responsibilities:
- Remove user’s cart and reservations.
- Return reserved stock to available stock if the product exists.
- Preserve the user’s existing orders.

Preconditions:
- User exists in the system.

Postconditions:
- User, their cart, and reservations are removed.
- Stock levels are updated if applicable.
- User’s orders remain unchanged.

Main Success Scenario:
1. Administrator requests to delete a user.
2. System removes the user’s cart and reservations.
3. For each reserved product:
   a. If the product exists in stock, system returns the reserved quantity to available stock.
   b. If the product no longer exists, system removes the reservation without stock adjustment.
4. System removes the user from the system.

Extensions:
1a. User does not exist → System ignores the request.

---

**Use Case: Add/Update Product**
Primary Actor: Administrator
Goal: Add a new product or update an existing product’s stock and price.

System Responsibilities:
- Validate product attributes (name, price, stock).
- Update stock and price if the product already exists.

Preconditions:
- System is operational.

Postconditions:
- Product is added or updated in the system.
- Stock and price reflect the latest values.

Main Success Scenario:
1. Administrator requests to add/update a product with name, price, and stock.
2. System checks if the product already exists.
   a. If it exists, system updates the stock (increases) and price.
   b. If it does not exist, system creates the product with the given attributes.

Extensions:
1a. Product name is empty or invalid → System ignores the request.
1b. Price or stock is negative → System ignores the request.

---

**Use Case: Delete Product**
Primary Actor: Administrator
Goal: Remove a product from the current available stock while preserving reservations and orders.

System Responsibilities:
- Remove product from available stock.
- Preserve existing reservations and orders.

Preconditions:
- Product exists in the system.

Postconditions:
- Product is removed from available stock.
- Reservations and orders involving the product remain unchanged.

Main Success Scenario:
1. Administrator requests to delete a product.
2. System removes the product from available stock.

Extensions:
1a. Product does not exist → System ignores the request.

---

**Use Case: Modify Cart**
Primary Actor: Customer
Goal: Add or remove products from the cart, updating reservations and stock accordingly.

System Responsibilities:
- Validate product availability and quantity.
- Update reservations and stock when products are added/removed.
- Ignore invalid operations (e.g., zero quantity, insufficient stock).

Preconditions:
- User exists in the system.
- Cart exists for the user.

Postconditions:
- Cart reflects the latest product quantities.
- Reservations and stock are updated if applicable.

Main Success Scenario (Add Product):
1. Customer requests to add a product with a specified quantity to their cart.
2. System checks if the product exists and has sufficient stock.
3. System updates the cart and creates/updates the reservation.
4. System deducts the reserved quantity from available stock.

Extensions:
2a. Product does not exist or stock is insufficient → System ignores the request.
2b. Quantity is zero or negative → System ignores the request.

Main Success Scenario (Remove Product):
1. Customer requests to remove a product with a specified quantity from their cart.
2. System checks if the product exists in the cart and the quantity is valid.
3. System reduces/removes the reservation.
   a. If the product exists in stock, system returns the quantity to available stock.
   b. If the product no longer exists, system removes the reservation without stock adjustment.

Extensions:
2a. Product does not exist in cart or quantity exceeds reserved amount → System ignores the request.

---

**Use Case: Perform Checkout**
Primary Actor: Customer
Goal: Convert the cart into an order, clearing reservations and generating a unique order ID.

System Responsibilities:
- Validate cart and product availability.
- Calculate order total using current prices.
- Create an order with status "pending" and a unique ID.
- Clear the cart and reservations.
- Ensure atomicity (all-or-nothing).

Preconditions:
- User exists in the system.
- Cart is not empty.

Postconditions:
- Order is created with status "pending" and a unique ID.
- Cart and reservations are cleared.
- Stock levels remain unchanged (already deducted during reservation).

Main Success Scenario:
1. Customer requests to checkout their cart.
2. System validates that all reserved products exist in stock.
3. System calculates the order total using current product prices.
4. System generates a unique order ID in the format "SK00001".
5. System creates an order with status "pending" and stores a snapshot of product prices.
6. System clears the cart and reservations.

Extensions:
2a. Any reserved product does not exist in stock → System fails checkout and no changes are applied.
1a. Cart is empty → System ignores the request.

---

**Use Case: Update Order Status**
Primary Actor: Administrator
Goal: Transition an order’s status following strict rules, including handling cancellations.

System Responsibilities:
- Validate status transitions.
- Handle stock adjustments for cancellations.
- Recreate products if necessary during cancellation.

Preconditions:
- Order exists in the system.

Postconditions:
- Order status is updated if the transition is valid.
- Stock levels are adjusted if the order is cancelled.

Main Success Scenario:
1. Administrator requests to update an order’s status.
2. System validates the transition (e.g., "pending" → "processing").
3. System updates the order status.

Extensions:
2a. Invalid transition (e.g., "delivered" → "processing") → System ignores the request.
2b. Transition to "cancelled":
   a. For each product in the order:
      i. If the product exists in stock, system increases available stock by the ordered quantity.
      ii. If the product no longer exists, system recreates it with the price stored in the order.
   b. System updates the order status to "cancelled".

Special Requirements:
- Order status transitions must follow the sequence: pending → processing → completed → shipped → delivered.
- "cancelled" is a terminal state and can be reached from any status.
- From "delivered", only "cancelled" is allowed.
===============================

- No prior state corruption exists (e.g., stock inconsistencies).

Postconditions:

**Use Case: Modify Cart**

Preconditions:
- User exists in the system.
- Cart exists for the user.

Postconditions:
- Cart reflects the latest product quantities.
- Reservations and stock are updated if applicable.

Main Success Scenario (Add Product):
1. Customer requests to add a product with a specified quantity to their cart.
2. System checks if the product exists and has sufficient stock.
3. System updates the cart and creates/updates the reservation.
4. System deducts the reserved quantity from available stock.

Extensions:
2a. Product does not exist or stock is insufficient → System ignores the request.
2b. Quantity is zero or negative → System ignores the request.

Main Success Scenario (Remove Product):
1. Customer requests to remove a product with a specified quantity from their cart.
2. System validates that the product exists in the cart and the quantity is valid (does not exceed reserved amount).
3. System reduces/removes the reservation.
   a. If the product exists in stock, system returns the quantity to available stock.
   b. If the product no longer exists, system removes the reservation without stock adjustment.

Extensions:
2a. Product does not exist in cart or quantity exceeds reserved amount → System ignores the request.

---

**Use Case: Perform Checkout**
Primary Actor: Customer
Goal: Convert the cart into an order, clearing reservations and generating a unique order ID.

System Responsibilities:
- Validate cart and product availability.
- Calculate order total using current prices.
- Create an order with status "pending" and a unique ID.
- Store a snapshot of product prices at the time of checkout.
- Clear the cart and reservations.
- Ensure atomicity (all-or-nothing).

Preconditions:
- User exists in the system.
- Cart is not empty.

Postconditions:
- Order is created with status "pending" and a unique ID.
- Cart and reservations are cleared.
- Stock levels remain unchanged (already deducted during reservation).

Main Success Scenario:
1. Customer requests to checkout their cart.
2. System validates that all reserved products exist in stock.
3. System calculates the order total using current product prices.
4. System generates a unique order ID in the format "SK00001".
5. System creates an order with status "pending", stores a snapshot of product prices, and clears the cart and reservations.
6. System ensures atomicity of the operation.

Extensions:
2a. Any reserved product does not exist in stock → System fails checkout and no changes are applied.
1a. Cart is empty → System ignores the request.

---

**Use Case: Update Order Status**
Primary Actor: Administrator
Goal: Transition an order’s status following strict rules, including handling cancellations and ensuring "cancelled" is a terminal state.

System Responsibilities:
- Validate status transitions.
- Handle stock adjustments for cancellations, including recreating products if necessary.
- Ensure "cancelled" is a terminal state and ignore further status changes.

Preconditions:
- Order exists in the system.

Postconditions:
- Order status is updated if the transition is valid.
- Stock levels are adjusted if the order is cancelled.

Main Success Scenario:
1. Administrator requests to update an order’s status.
2. System validates the transition (e.g., "pending" → "processing").
3. If transitioning to "cancelled":
   a. For each product in the order:
      i. If the product exists in stock, system increases available stock by the ordered quantity.
      ii. If the product no longer exists, system recreates it with the price stored in the order.
   b. System updates the order status to "cancelled" and ensures no further status changes are allowed.
4. System updates the order status if the transition is valid.

Extensions:
2a. Invalid transition (e.g., "delivered" → "processing") → System ignores the request.
2b. Attempt to change status from "cancelled" → System ignores the request.

Special Requirements:
- Order status transitions must follow the sequence: pending → processing → completed → shipped → delivered.
- "cancelled" is a terminal state and can be reached from any status.
- From "delivered", only "cancelled" is allowed.

---

Here's the revised Use Case with corrections applied based on the valid review issues:

---

**Use Case: Manage E-Shop Backend System**
Primary Actor: User (Administrator/Customer)
Goal: Manage users, products, carts, reservations, and orders in an e-shop backend system while ensuring correct handling of current available stock.

System Responsibilities:
- Manage user accounts, including creation and deletion.
- Maintain product catalog with attributes (name, price, stock).
- Handle cart operations (add/remove products) and reservations.
- Process checkout to create orders with atomicity.
- Update order statuses following strict transition rules.
- Ensure stock consistency during reservations, checkouts, and cancellations.
- Generate globally unique and sequential order IDs across the entire system without reuse, even if orders are cancelled.
- Provide access to current system state (users, products, stock, carts, orders).

Preconditions:
- The e-shop backend system is initialized and operational.
- No prior state corruption exists (e.g., stock inconsistencies).

Postconditions:
- System state reflects all successful operations (user/product management, cart modifications, checkouts, status updates).
- Stock levels, reservations, and orders remain consistent.
- Order IDs are generated sequentially and uniquely without reuse.

---

**Use Case: Modify Cart**

- If a product no longer exists in stock during removal, remove the reservation without affecting stock.

Preconditions:
- User exists in the system.
- Cart exists for the user.

Postconditions:
- Cart reflects the latest product quantities.
- Reservations and stock are updated if applicable.

Main Success Scenario (Add Product):
1. Customer requests to add a product with a specified quantity to their cart.
2. System checks if the product exists and has sufficient stock.
3. System updates the cart and creates/updates the reservation.
4. System deducts the reserved quantity from available stock.

2b. Quantity is zero or negative → System ignores the request.

Main Success Scenario (Remove Product):
1. Customer requests to remove a product with a specified quantity from their cart.
2. System validates that the product exists in the cart and the quantity is valid (does not exceed reserved amount).
3. System reduces/removes the reservation.
   a. If the product exists in stock, system returns the quantity to available stock.
   b. If the product no longer exists, system removes the reservation without stock adjustment.

Extensions:
2a. Product does not exist in cart or quantity exceeds reserved amount → System ignores the request.

---

**Use Case: Perform Checkout**
Primary Actor: Customer
Goal: Convert the cart into an order, clearing reservations and generating a unique order ID.

System Responsibilities:
- Validate cart and product availability.
- Calculate order total using current prices.
- Create an order with status "pending" and a unique ID.
- Store a snapshot of product prices at the time of checkout.
- Clear the cart and reservations.
- Ensure atomicity (all-or-nothing) of the checkout operation.

Preconditions:
- User exists in the system.
- Cart is not empty.

Postconditions:
- Order is created with status "pending" and a unique ID.
- Cart and reservations are cleared.
- Stock levels remain unchanged (already deducted during reservation).

Main Success Scenario:
1. Customer requests to checkout their cart.
2. System validates that all reserved products exist in stock.
3. System calculates the order total using current product prices.
4. System generates a unique order ID in the format "SK00001".
5. System creates an order with status "pending", stores a snapshot of product prices, and clears the cart and reservations.
6. System ensures atomicity of the operation.

Extensions:
2a. Any reserved product does not exist in stock → System fails checkout and no changes are applied.
1a. Cart is empty → System ignores the request.

---

**Use Case: Update Order Status**
Primary Actor: Administrator
Goal: Transition an order’s status following strict rules, including handling cancellations and ensuring "cancelled" is a terminal state.

System Responsibilities:
- Validate status transitions.
- Handle stock adjustments for cancellations, including recreating products if necessary using the price stored in the order snapshot.
- Ensure "cancelled" is a terminal state and ignore further status changes.

Preconditions:
- Order exists in the system.

Postconditions:
- Order status is updated if the transition is valid.
- Stock levels are adjusted if the order is cancelled.

Main Success Scenario:
1. Administrator requests to update an order’s status.
2. System validates the transition (e.g., "pending" → "processing").
3. If transitioning to "cancelled":
   a. For each product in the order:
      i. If the product exists in stock, system increases available stock by the ordered quantity.
      ii. If the product no longer exists, system recreates it with the price stored in the order snapshot.
   b. System updates the order status to "cancelled" and ensures no further status changes are allowed.
4. System updates the order status if the transition is valid.

Extensions:
2a. Invalid transition (e.g., "delivered" → "processing") → System ignores the request.
2b. Attempt to change status from "cancelled" → System ignores the request.

Special Requirements:
- Order status transitions must follow the sequence: pending → processing → completed → shipped → delivered.
- "cancelled" is a terminal state and can be reached from any status.
- From "delivered", only "cancelled" is allowed.
- When an order is cancelled, recreated products must use the price stored in the order snapshot, not the current price.
- "cancelled" is a terminal state, and any further status changes must be ignored.

    "Use Case 'Update Order Status' does not explicitly state that the system must ignore further status changes after an order is marked as 'cancelled' (terminal state), though this is implied in the extensions. The User Task explicitly requires this, so it should be clearly stated in the main success scenario or system responsibilities.",
    "Use Case 'Modify Cart' does not explicitly state that adding a product to the cart that is already reserved should only modify and increase the number of given products in the reservation, as required by the User Task. This is only implied in the extensions but should be explicitly stated in the main success scenario.",
    "Use Case 'Add/Update Product' does not explicitly state that the system must ignore requests with negative stock values, though it mentions negative prices. The User Task does not explicitly forbid negative stock, but the system should ignore invalid inputs, so this should be clarified or added as an extension.",
    "Use Case 'Perform Checkout' does not explicitly state that the system must ensure the order ID is globally unique and sequential without reuse, even if orders are cancelled. This is mentioned in the postconditions of the overarching use case but should be reiterated in the 'Perform Checkout' use case for clarity."
  ]
}
===============================


===== OUTPUT_generate_use_case OUTPUT =====
Here's the revised Use Case with corrections applied based on the valid review issues:

---

**Use Case: Manage E-Shop Backend System**
Primary Actor: User (Administrator/Customer)
Goal: Manage users, products, carts, reservations, and orders in an e-shop backend system while ensuring correct handling of current available stock.

System Responsibilities:
- Manage user accounts, including creation and deletion.
- Maintain product catalog with attributes (name, price, stock).
- Handle cart operations (add/remove products) and reservations.
- Process checkout to create orders with atomicity.
- Update order statuses following strict transition rules, including handling cancellations by recreating products with the price stored in the order snapshot if necessary.
- Ensure stock consistency during reservations, checkouts, and cancellations.
- Generate globally unique and sequential order IDs across the entire system without reuse, even if orders are cancelled.
- Provide access to current system state (users, products, stock, carts, orders).

Preconditions:
- The e-shop backend system is initialized and operational.
- No prior state corruption exists (e.g., stock inconsistencies).

Postconditions:
- System state reflects all successful operations (user/product management, cart modifications, checkouts, status updates).
- Stock levels, reservations, and orders remain consistent.
- Order IDs are generated sequentially and uniquely without reuse.

---

**Use Case: Create User**
Primary Actor: Administrator
Goal: Add a new user to the system with a unique ID and an empty cart.

System Responsibilities:
- Validate uniqueness of user ID.
- Create an empty cart for the new user.

Preconditions:
- System is operational.

Postconditions:
- New user is added to the system.
- User has an empty cart.

Main Success Scenario:
1. Administrator requests to create a user with a specified ID.
2. System validates that the ID is unique.
3. System creates the user with the given ID.
4. System initializes an empty cart for the user.

Extensions:
2a. User ID already exists → System ignores the request.

---

**Use Case: Delete User**
Primary Actor: Administrator
Goal: Remove a user from the system, including their cart and reservations, while maintaining stock consistency and preserving existing orders.

System Responsibilities:
- Remove user’s cart and reservations.
- Return reserved stock to available stock if the product exists.
- Preserve the user’s existing orders without any changes.
- Ensure stock levels are updated if applicable.

Preconditions:
- User exists in the system.

Postconditions:
- User, their cart, and reservations are removed.
- Stock levels are updated if applicable.
- User’s orders remain unchanged.

Main Success Scenario:
1. Administrator requests to delete a user.
2. System removes the user’s cart and reservations.
3. For each reserved product:
   a. If the product exists in stock, system returns the reserved quantity to available stock.
   b. If the product no longer exists, system removes the reservation without stock adjustment.
4. System removes the user from the system while preserving their existing orders.

Extensions:
1a. User does not exist → System ignores the request.

---

**Use Case: Add/Update Product**
Primary Actor: Administrator
Goal: Add a new product or update an existing product’s stock and price, allowing zero stock.

System Responsibilities:
- Validate product attributes (name, price, stock).
- Update stock and price if the product already exists.
- Allow zero stock for products.
- Ignore requests with empty or invalid product names, negative prices, or negative stock values.

Preconditions:
- System is operational.

Postconditions:
- Product is added or updated in the system.
- Stock and price reflect the latest values.

Main Success Scenario:
1. Administrator requests to add/update a product with name, price, and stock.
2. System checks if the product already exists.
   a. If it exists, system updates the stock (increases) and price.
   b. If it does not exist, system creates the product with the given attributes, allowing zero stock.

Extensions:
1a. Product name is empty or invalid → System ignores the request.
1b. Price is negative → System ignores the request.
1c. Stock is negative → System ignores the request.

---

**Use Case: Delete Product**
Primary Actor: Administrator
Goal: Remove a product from the current available stock while preserving existing reservations and orders.

System Responsibilities:
- Remove product from available stock.
- Preserve existing reservations and orders involving the product.

Preconditions:
- Product exists in the system.

Postconditions:
- Product is removed from available stock.
- Reservations and orders involving the product remain unchanged.

Main Success Scenario:
1. Administrator requests to delete a product.
2. System removes the product from available stock while preserving existing reservations and orders.

Extensions:
1a. Product does not exist → System ignores the request.

---

**Use Case: Modify Cart**
Primary Actor: Customer
Goal: Add or remove products from the cart, updating reservations and stock accordingly.

System Responsibilities:
- Validate product availability and quantity.
- Update reservations and stock when products are added/removed.
- If a product is already reserved, modify and increase the number of given products in the reservation.
- Ignore invalid operations (e.g., zero quantity, insufficient stock, product not in cart, quantity exceeds reserved amount).
- If a product no longer exists in stock during removal, remove the reservation without affecting stock.

Preconditions:
- User exists in the system.
- Cart exists for the user.

Postconditions:
- Cart reflects the latest product quantities.
- Reservations and stock are updated if applicable.

Main Success Scenario (Add Product):
1. Customer requests to add a product with a specified quantity to their cart.
2. System checks if the product exists and has sufficient stock.
3. If the product is already reserved, system modifies and increases the number of given products in the reservation.
4. System updates the cart and creates/updates the reservation.
5. System deducts the reserved quantity from available stock.

Extensions:
2a. Product does not exist or stock is insufficient → System ignores the request.
2b. Quantity is zero or negative → System ignores the request.

Main Success Scenario (Remove Product):
1. Customer requests to remove a product with a specified quantity from their cart.
2. System validates that the product exists in the cart and the quantity is valid (does not exceed reserved amount).
3. System reduces/removes the reservation.
   a. If the product exists in stock, system returns the quantity to available stock.
   b. If the product no longer exists, system removes the reservation without stock adjustment.

Extensions:
2a. Product does not exist in cart or quantity exceeds reserved amount → System ignores the request.

---

**Use Case: Perform Checkout**
Primary Actor: Customer
Goal: Convert the cart into an order, clearing reservations and generating a unique order ID.

System Responsibilities:
- Validate cart and product availability.
- Calculate order total using current prices.
- Create an order with status "pending" and a globally unique and sequential order ID without reuse, even if orders are cancelled.
- Store a snapshot of product prices at the time of checkout.
- Clear the cart and reservations.
- Ensure atomicity (all-or-nothing) of the checkout operation.

Preconditions:
- User exists in the system.
- Cart is not empty.

Postconditions:
- Order is created with status "pending" and a unique ID.
- Cart and reservations are cleared.
- Stock levels remain unchanged (already deducted during reservation).

Main Success Scenario:
1. Customer requests to checkout their cart.
2. System validates that all reserved products exist in stock.
3. System calculates the order total using current product prices.
4. System generates a unique and sequential order ID in the format "SK00001" without reuse, even if orders are cancelled.
5. System creates an order with status "pending", stores a snapshot of product prices, and clears the cart and reservations.
6. System ensures atomicity of the operation.

Extensions:
2a. Any reserved product does not exist in stock → System fails checkout and no changes are applied.
1a. Cart is empty → System ignores the request.

---

**Use Case: Update Order Status**
Primary Actor: Administrator
Goal: Transition an order’s status following strict rules, including handling cancellations and ensuring "cancelled" is a terminal state.

System Responsibilities:
- Validate status transitions.
- Handle stock adjustments for cancellations, including recreating products if necessary using the price stored in the order snapshot.
- Ensure "cancelled" is a terminal state and ignore further status changes.

Preconditions:
- Order exists in the system.

Postconditions:
- Order status is updated if the transition is valid.
- Stock levels are adjusted if the order is cancelled.

Main Success Scenario:
1. Administrator requests to update an order’s status.
2. System validates the transition (e.g., "pending" → "processing").
3. If transitioning to "cancelled":
   a. For each product in the order:
      i. If the product exists in stock, system increases available stock by the ordered quantity.
      ii. If the product no longer exists, system recreates it with the price stored in the order snapshot.
   b. System updates the order status to "cancelled" and ensures no further status changes are allowed.
4. System updates the order status if the transition is valid and ignores further status changes after the order is marked as "cancelled".

Extensions:
2a. Invalid transition (e.g., "delivered" → "processing") → System ignores the request.
2b. Attempt to change status from "cancelled" → System ignores the request.

Special Requirements:
- Order status transitions must follow the sequence: pending → processing → completed → shipped → delivered.
- "cancelled" is a terminal state and can be reached from any status.
- From "delivered", only "cancelled" is allowed.
- When an order is cancelled, recreated products must use the price stored in the order snapshot, not the current price.
- "cancelled" is a terminal state, and any further status changes must be ignored.
===============================

{
  "score": 0.85,
  "issues": [
    "Missing persistent state for tracking order ID sequence format ('SK' prefix with 5-digit sequential number). The 'next_order_id' attribute is defined as an integer, but there is no mechanism to ensure the 'SK00001' format is enforced as required by the Use Case.",
    "The 'perform_checkout' method does not explicitly mention storing a snapshot of product prices at the time of checkout, which is required by the Use Case for handling cancellations and recreating products with the correct price.",
    "The 'update_order_status' method does not explicitly state that 'cancelled' is a terminal state and that further status changes must be ignored, though this is implied in the special requirements.",
    "The 'add_to_cart' method does not explicitly mention that reserved quantities must not be available to other users, though this is implied by the stock deduction step.",
    "The 'delete_user' method does not explicitly state that existing orders of the user must remain unaffected, though this is implied in the special requirements.",
    "The 'remove_from_cart' method does not explicitly mention that the operation must be ignored if the product no longer exists in the stock system, though this is implied by the steps.",
    "The 'update_order_status' method does not explicitly mention that recreated products must use the price stored in the order snapshot, not the current price, though this is implied in the special requirements."
  ]

===== OUTPUT_review_architecture OUTPUT =====
{
  "score": 0.85,
  "issues": [
    "Missing attribute to track the current available stock separately from total product stock. The architecture mentions 'available stock' in multiple methods (e.g., add_to_cart, remove_from_cart, perform_checkout) but does not explicitly model it as a distinct attribute. This could lead to inconsistencies in stock management.",
    "The `perform_checkout` method does not explicitly mention the atomicity mechanism (e.g., transaction rollback) in its steps, despite the special requirements emphasizing atomicity. This could lead to partial updates if an error occurs during checkout.",
    "The `update_order_status` method does not explicitly state that the order snapshot prices are used for recreating products in the steps, only in the special requirements. This could lead to incorrect implementation where current prices are used instead of snapshot prices.",
    "The `delete_user` method does not explicitly mention the preservation of existing orders in its steps, only in the special requirements. This could lead to accidental deletion of orders during implementation.",
    "The `add_or_update_product` method does not explicitly mention that zero stock is allowed in its steps, only in the special requirements. This could lead to incorrect validation during implementation.",
    "The `remove_from_cart` method does not explicitly mention that the operation should be ignored if the product no longer exists in the stock system in its steps, only in the special requirements. This could lead to inconsistent behavior during implementation.",
    "The `orders` attribute in the `EShopSystem` class does not explicitly include the order snapshot prices in its description, which are critical for handling cancellations. This could lead to missing data during order status updates."
  ]
}

===== OUTPUT_review_architecture OUTPUT =====
{
  "score": 0.85,
  "issues": [
    "Missing attribute to track 'available_stock' separately from 'total_stock' in the product data. The architecture mentions 'total stock' and 'available stock' in descriptions but only has a single 'stock' attribute in the product data structure, which could lead to inconsistencies in stock management.",
    "The 'add_or_update_product' method does not explicitly state that it should update both 'total_stock' and 'available_stock' when a product already exists, which could lead to incorrect stock calculations during reservations and checkouts.",
    "The 'delete_user' method does not explicitly state that it should check if the product exists in the stock system before attempting to return reserved quantities, which could lead to errors if the product was deleted after being reserved.",
    "The 'remove_from_cart' method's special requirements do not explicitly state that the operation should be ignored if the product no longer exists in the stock system, which could lead to inconsistencies in the reservation logic.",
    "The 'update_order_status' method does not explicitly state that the price snapshot from the order should be used to recreate products when transitioning to 'cancelled', which could lead to incorrect product recreation during cancellations.",
    "The 'perform_checkout' method does not explicitly state that the order ID generation must be thread-safe if the system is used in a multi-threaded environment, which could lead to duplicate order IDs.",
    "The 'reservations' attribute description does not clarify whether it tracks the reserved quantity per product per user or just the existence of a reservation, which could lead to ambiguities in stock management."
  ]
}

===== generate_single_method OUTPUT =====
Generated code for EShopSystem.delete_user: 
def delete_user(self, user_id):
    if user_id not in self.users:
        return

    # Process reservations
    if user_id in self.reservations:
        for product_name, quantity in self.reservations[user_id].items():
            if product_name in self.products:
                self.products[product_name]['available_stock'] += quantity
        del self.reservations[user_id]

    # Remove cart
    if user_id in self.carts:
        del self.carts[user_id]

    # Remove user
    del self.users[user_id]
===============================


===== generate_single_method OUTPUT =====
Generated code for EShopSystem.add_or_update_product: 
def add_or_update_product(self, name: str, price: float, stock: int) -> None:
    if not name or price < 0 or stock < 0:
        return

    if name in self.products:
        product = self.products[name]
        product['price'] = price
        product['total_stock'] = stock
        product['available_stock'] = stock
    else:
        self.products[name] = {
            'price': price,
            'total_stock': stock,
            'available_stock': stock
        }

def delete_product(self, name):
    if name not in self.products:
        return

    del self.products[name]

    for user_id in self.reservations:
        if name in self.reservations[user_id]:
            del self.reservations[user_id][name]

    for user_id in self.carts:
        if name in self.carts[user_id]:
            del self.carts[user_id][name]

===== generate_single_method OUTPUT =====
Generated code for EShopSystem.add_to_cart: 
def add_to_cart(self, user_id, product_name, quantity):
    # Check if user and product exist, and quantity is valid
    if (user_id not in self.users or
        product_name not in self.products or
        quantity <= 0):
        return

    # Check if product has sufficient available stock
    if self.products[product_name]['available_stock'] < quantity:
        return

    # Initialize cart and reservations if not present
    if user_id not in self.carts:
        self.carts[user_id] = {}
    if user_id not in self.reservations:
        self.reservations[user_id] = {}

    # Update cart quantity
    if product_name in self.carts[user_id]:
        self.carts[user_id][product_name] += quantity
    else:
        self.carts[user_id][product_name] = quantity

    # Update reservations and available stock
    if product_name in self.reservations[user_id]:
        self.reservations[user_id][product_name] += quantity
    else:
        self.reservations[user_id][product_name] = quantity

    self.products[product_name]['available_stock'] -= quantity
===============================


===== generate_single_method OUTPUT =====
Generated code for EShopSystem.remove_from_cart: 
def remove_from_cart(self, user_id: str, product_name: str, quantity: int) -> None:
    # Check if the user exists and has the product in their cart with sufficient quantity
    if (user_id not in self.users or
        product_name not in self.carts.get(user_id, {}) or
        quantity <= 0 or
        self.carts[user_id][product_name] < quantity):
        return

    # Reduce or remove the product from the cart
    if self.carts[user_id][product_name] == quantity:
        del self.carts[user_id][product_name]
    else:
        self.carts[user_id][product_name] -= quantity

    # Check if the product exists in the stock system
    if product_name in self.products:
        # Return the quantity to available stock
        self.products[product_name]['available_stock'] += quantity

    # Remove the reservation if it exists
    if (user_id in self.reservations and
        product_name in self.reservations[user_id] and
        self.reservations[user_id][product_name] >= quantity):
        if self.reservations[user_id][product_name] == quantity:
            del self.reservations[user_id][product_name]
        else:
            self.reservations[user_id][product_name] -= quantity
===============================


===== generate_single_method OUTPUT =====
Generated code for EShopSystem.perform_checkout: 
def perform_checkout(self, user_id):
    # Check if user exists and has a non-empty cart
    if user_id not in self.users or user_id not in self.carts or not self.carts[user_id]:
        return None

    # Validate all reserved products exist in stock
    for product_name in self.carts[user_id]:
        if product_name not in self.products:
            return None

    # Calculate order total using current prices
    order_total = 0.0
    price_snapshot = {}
    for product_name, quantity in self.carts[user_id].items():
        order_total += self.products[product_name]['price'] * quantity
        price_snapshot[product_name] = self.products[product_name]['price']

    # Generate order ID in thread-safe manner
    with threading.Lock():
        order_id = f"SK{self.next_order_sequence:05d}"
        self.next_order_sequence += 1

    # Create order with status 'pending'
    self.orders[order_id] = {
        'status': 'pending',
        'products': self.carts[user_id].copy(),
        'price_snapshot': price_snapshot,
        'total': order_total,
        'user_id': user_id
    }

    # Clear cart and reservations atomically
    self.carts[user_id].clear()
    if user_id in self.reservations:
        self.reservations[user_id].clear()

    return order_id
===============================


===== generate_single_method OUTPUT =====
Generated code for EShopSystem.update_order_status: 
def update_order_status(self, order_id: str, new_status: str) -> None:
    if order_id not in self.orders:
        return

    order = self.orders[order_id]
    current_status = order['status']

    if current_status == 'cancelled':
        return

    valid_transitions = {
        'pending': ['processing'],
        'processing': ['completed'],
        'completed': ['shipped'],
        'shipped': ['delivered'],
        'delivered': ['cancelled'],
        'cancelled': []
    }

    if new_status not in valid_transitions[current_status]:
        return

    if new_status == 'cancelled':
        for product_name, quantity in order['products'].items():
            if product_name in self.products:
                self.products[product_name]['available_stock'] += quantity
            else:
                price_snapshot = order['price_snapshot'].get(product_name, 0.0)
                self.products[product_name] = {
                    'price': price_snapshot,
                    'total_stock': quantity,
                    'available_stock': quantity
                }

    order['status'] = new_status
===============================

Generated code for EShopSystem.get_system_state: 
def get_system_state(self) -> dict:
    """Provides access to the current system state, including users, products, stock, carts, and orders."""
    return {
        "users": self.users,
        "products": self.products,
        "carts": self.carts,
        "reservations": self.reservations,
        "orders": self.orders,
        "next_order_sequence": self.next_order_sequence
    }

def remove_from_cart(self, user_id: str, product_name: str, quantity: int) -> None:
    # Check if the user exists and has the product in their cart with sufficient quantity
    if (user_id not in self.users or
        product_name not in self.carts.get(user_id, {}) or
        quantity <= 0 or
        self.carts[user_id][product_name] < quantity):
        return

    # Remove the reservation if it exists
    if (user_id in self.reservations and
        product_name in self.reservations[user_id] and
        self.reservations[user_id][product_name] >= quantity):
        if self.reservations[user_id][product_name] == quantity:
            del self.reservations[user_id][product_name]

        del self.carts[user_id][product_name]
    else:
        self.carts[user_id][product_name] -= quantity

    # Check if the product exists in the stock system
    if product_name in self.products:
        # Return the quantity to available stock
        self.products[product_name]['available_stock'] += quantity

===== regenerate_single_method OUTPUT =====
Regenerated code for EShopSystem.perform_checkout: 
def perform_checkout(self, user_id):
    # Check if user exists and has a non-empty cart
    if user_id not in self.users or user_id not in self.carts or not self.carts[user_id]:
        return None

    # Validate all reserved products exist in stock and have sufficient available quantity
    for product_name, quantity in self.carts[user_id].items():
        if product_name not in self.products or self.products[product_name]['available_stock'] < quantity:
            return None

    # Calculate order total using current prices
    order_total = 0.0
    price_snapshot = {}
    for product_name, quantity in self.carts[user_id].items():
        order_total += self.products[product_name]['price'] * quantity
        price_snapshot[product_name] = self.products[product_name]['price']

    # Generate order ID in thread-safe manner
    with threading.Lock():
        order_id = f"SK{self.next_order_sequence:05d}"
        self.next_order_sequence += 1

    # Create order with status 'pending'
    self.orders[order_id] = {
        'status': 'pending',
        'products': self.carts[user_id].copy(),
        'price_snapshot': price_snapshot,
        'total': order_total,
        'user_id': user_id
    }

    # Clear cart and reservations atomically
    self.carts[user_id].clear()
    if user_id in self.reservations:
        self.reservations[user_id].clear()

    return order_id
===============================


===== regenerate_single_method OUTPUT =====
Regenerated code for EShopSystem.update_order_status: 
def update_order_status(self, order_id: str, new_status: str) -> None:
    if order_id not in self.orders:
        return

    order = self.orders[order_id]
    current_status = order['status']

    if current_status == 'cancelled':
        return

    valid_transitions = {
        'pending': ['processing', 'cancelled'],
        'processing': ['completed', 'cancelled'],
        'completed': ['shipped', 'cancelled'],
        'shipped': ['delivered', 'cancelled'],
        'delivered': ['cancelled'],
        'cancelled': []
    }

    if new_status not in valid_transitions[current_status]:
        return

    if new_status == 'cancelled':
        for product_name, quantity in order['products'].items():
            if product_name in self.products:
                self.products[product_name]['available_stock'] += quantity
            else:
                price_snapshot = order['price_snapshot'].get(product_name, 0.0)
                self.products[product_name] = {
                    'price': price_snapshot,
                    'total_stock': quantity,
                    'available_stock': quantity
                }

    order['status'] = new_status
===============================

def get_system_state(self) -> dict:
    """Provides access to the current system state, including users, products, stock, carts, and orders."""
    return {
        "users": self.users,
        "products": {name: {"price": data["price"], "available_stock": data["available_stock"]}
                   for name, data in self.products.items()},
        "carts": self.carts,
        "reservations": self.reservations,
        "orders": self.orders,
        "next_order_sequence": self.next_order_sequence
    }

===== regenerate_single_method OUTPUT =====
Regenerated code for EShopSystem.add_to_cart: 
def add_to_cart(self, user_id, product_name, quantity):
    # Check if user and product exist, and quantity is valid
    if (user_id not in self.users or
        product_name not in self.products or
        quantity <= 0):
        return

    # Check if product has sufficient available stock
    if self.products[product_name]['available_stock'] < quantity:
        return

    # Initialize cart and reservations if not present
    if user_id not in self.carts:
        self.carts[user_id] = {}
    if user_id not in self.reservations:
        self.reservations[user_id] = {}

    # Update cart quantity
    if product_name in self.carts[user_id]:
        self.carts[user_id][product_name] += quantity
    else:
        self.carts[user_id][product_name] = quantity

    # Update reservations and available stock
    if product_name in self.reservations[user_id]:
        self.reservations[user_id][product_name] += quantity
    else:
        self.reservations[user_id][product_name] = quantity

    self.products[product_name]['available_stock'] -= quantity
===============================


===== regenerate_single_method OUTPUT =====
Regenerated code for EShopSystem.remove_from_cart: 
def remove_from_cart(self, user_id: str, product_name: str, quantity: int) -> None:
    # Check if the product exists in the stock system
    product_exists_in_stock = product_name in self.products

    # Check if the user exists and has the product in their cart with sufficient quantity
    if (user_id not in self.users or
        product_name not in self.carts.get(user_id, {}) or
        quantity <= 0 or
        self.carts[user_id][product_name] < quantity):
        return

    # If the product no longer exists in the stock system, remove the reservation and ignore the operation
    if not product_exists_in_stock:
        if (user_id in self.reservations and
            product_name in self.reservations[user_id] and
            self.reservations[user_id][product_name] >= quantity):
            if self.reservations[user_id][product_name] == quantity:
                del self.reservations[user_id][product_name]
            else:
                self.reservations[user_id][product_name] -= quantity
        return

    # Remove the reservation if it exists
    if (user_id in self.reservations and
        product_name in self.reservations[user_id] and
        self.reservations[user_id][product_name] >= quantity):
        if self.reservations[user_id][product_name] == quantity:
            del self.reservations[user_id][product_name]
        else:
            self.reservations[user_id][product_name] -= quantity

    # Reduce or remove the product from the cart
    if self.carts[user_id][product_name] == quantity:
        del self.carts[user_id][product_name]
    else:
        self.carts[user_id][product_name] -= quantity

    # Return the quantity to available stock
    self.products[product_name]['available_stock'] += quantity

===== regenerate_single_method OUTPUT =====
Regenerated code for EShopSystem.perform_checkout: 
def perform_checkout(self, user_id):
    # Check if user exists and has a non-empty cart
    if user_id not in self.users or user_id not in self.carts or not self.carts[user_id]:
        return None

    # Validate all reserved products exist in stock and have sufficient available quantity
    for product_name, quantity in self.carts[user_id].items():
        if product_name not in self.products or self.products[product_name]['available_stock'] < quantity:
            return None

    # Calculate order total using current prices
    order_total = 0.0
    price_snapshot = {}
    for product_name, quantity in self.carts[user_id].items():
        order_total += self.products[product_name]['price'] * quantity
        price_snapshot[product_name] = self.products[product_name]['price']

    # Generate order ID in thread-safe manner
    with threading.Lock():
        order_id = f"SK{self.next_order_sequence:05d}"
        self.next_order_sequence += 1

    # Create order with status 'pending'
    order_data = {
        'status': 'pending',
        'products': self.carts[user_id].copy(),
        'price_snapshot': price_snapshot,
        'total': order_total,
        'user_id': user_id
    }

    # Store cart and reservations state for potential rollback
    cart_backup = self.carts[user_id].copy()
    reservations_backup = self.reservations.get(user_id, {}).copy()

    try:
        # Clear cart and reservations
        self.carts[user_id].clear()
        if user_id in self.reservations:
            self.reservations[user_id].clear()

        # Only after successful clearing, add the order
        self.orders[order_id] = order_data
        return order_id
    except:
        # Rollback changes if any error occurs
        self.carts[user_id] = cart_backup
        if user_id in self.reservations:
            self.reservations[user_id] = reservations_backup
        return None
===============================

Regenerated code for EShopSystem.get_system_state: 
def get_system_state(self) -> dict:
    """Provides access to the current system state, including users, products, stock, carts, and orders."""
    return {
        "users": self.users,
        "products": {name: {"price": data["price"], "total_stock": data["total_stock"], "available_stock": data["available_stock"]}
                   for name, data in self.products.items()},
        "carts": self.carts,
        "reservations": self.reservations,
        "orders": self.orders,
        "next_order_sequence": self.next_order_sequence
    }

def remove_from_cart(self, user_id: str, product_name: str, quantity: int) -> None:
    # Check if the product exists in the stock system
    product_exists_in_stock = product_name in self.products

    # Check if the user exists and has the product in their cart with sufficient quantity
    if (user_id not in self.users or
        product_name not in self.carts.get(user_id, {}) or
        quantity <= 0 or

    if self.carts[user_id][product_name] == quantity:
        del self.carts[user_id][product_name]
    else:
        self.carts[user_id][product_name] -= quantity

    # Return the quantity to available stock
    self.products[product_name]['available_stock'] += quantity

===== regenerate_single_method OUTPUT =====
Regenerated code for EShopSystem.perform_checkout: 
def perform_checkout(self, user_id):
    # Check if user exists and has a non-empty cart
    if user_id not in self.users or user_id not in self.carts or not self.carts[user_id]:
        return None

    # Validate all reserved products exist in stock and have sufficient available quantity
    for product_name, quantity in self.carts[user_id].items():
        if product_name not in self.products or self.products[product_name]['available_stock'] < quantity:
            return None

    # Calculate order total using current prices
    order_total = 0.0
    price_snapshot = {}
    for product_name, quantity in self.carts[user_id].items():
        order_total += self.products[product_name]['price'] * quantity
        price_snapshot[product_name] = self.products[product_name]['price']

    # Generate order ID in thread-safe manner
    with threading.Lock():
        order_id = f"SK{self.next_order_sequence:05d}"
        self.next_order_sequence += 1

    # Create order with status 'pending'
    order_data = {
        'status': 'pending',
        'products': self.carts[user_id].copy(),
        'price_snapshot': price_snapshot,
        'total': order_total,
        'user_id': user_id
    }

    # Store cart, reservations, and stock state for potential rollback
    cart_backup = self.carts[user_id].copy()
    reservations_backup = self.reservations.get(user_id, {}).copy()
    stock_backup = {}
    for product_name, quantity in self.carts[user_id].items():
        stock_backup[product_name] = self.products[product_name]['available_stock']

    try:
        # Deduct stock from available_stock
        for product_name, quantity in self.carts[user_id].items():
            self.products[product_name]['available_stock'] -= quantity

        # Clear cart and reservations
        self.carts[user_id].clear()
        if user_id in self.reservations:
            self.reservations[user_id].clear()

        # Only after successful clearing, add the order
        self.orders[order_id] = order_data
        return order_id
    except:
        # Rollback changes if any error occurs
        self.carts[user_id] = cart_backup
        if user_id in self.reservations:
            self.reservations[user_id] = reservations_backup
        for product_name, quantity in stock_backup.items():
            self.products[product_name]['available_stock'] = quantity
        return None
===============================

                   for name, data in self.products.items()},
        "carts": self.carts,
        "reservations": self.reservations,
        "orders": self.orders,
        "next_order_sequence": self.next_order_sequence
    }

def delete_user(self, user_id):

        return

    # Process reservations
    if user_id in self.reservations:
        for product_name, quantity in self.reservations[user_id].items():

    # Remove user (orders are preserved as per specification)

Regenerated code for EShopSystem.perform_checkout: 
def perform_checkout(self, user_id):
    # Check if user exists and has a non-empty cart
    if user_id not in self.users or user_id not in self.carts or not self.carts[user_id]:
        return None

    # Validate all reserved products exist in stock and have sufficient available quantity
    for product_name, quantity in self.carts[user_id].items():
        if product_name not in self.products or self.products[product_name]['available_stock'] < quantity:
            return None

    # Calculate order total using current prices
    order_total = 0.0
    price_snapshot = {}
    for product_name, quantity in self.carts[user_id].items():
        order_total += self.products[product_name]['price'] * quantity
        price_snapshot[product_name] = self.products[product_name]['price']

    # Generate order ID in thread-safe manner
    with threading.Lock():
        order_id = f"SK{self.next_order_sequence:05d}"
        self.next_order_sequence += 1

    # Create order with status 'pending'
    order_data = {
        'status': 'pending',
        'products': self.carts[user_id].copy(),
        'price_snapshot': price_snapshot,
        'total': order_total,
        'user_id': user_id
    }

    # Store cart, reservations, and stock state for potential rollback
    cart_backup = self.carts[user_id].copy()
    reservations_backup = self.reservations.get(user_id, {}).copy()
    stock_backup = {}
    for product_name, quantity in self.carts[user_id].items():
        stock_backup[product_name] = self.products[product_name]['available_stock']

    try:
        # Deduct stock from available_stock
        for product_name, quantity in self.carts[user_id].items():
            self.products[product_name]['available_stock'] -= quantity

        # Clear cart and reservations
        self.carts[user_id].clear()
        if user_id in self.reservations:
            self.reservations[user_id].clear()

        # Only after successful clearing, add the order
        self.orders[order_id] = order_data
        return order_id
    except:
        # Rollback changes if any error occurs
        self.carts[user_id] = cart_backup
        if user_id in self.reservations:
            self.reservations[user_id] = reservations_backup
        for product_name, quantity in stock_backup.items():
            self.products[product_name]['available_stock'] = quantity
        return None

def update_order_status(self, order_id: str, new_status: str) -> None:
    if order_id not in self.orders:
        return

    order = self.orders[order_id]
    current_status = order['status']

    if current_status == 'cancelled':
        return

    valid_transitions = {
        'pending': ['processing', 'cancelled'],
        'processing': ['completed', 'cancelled'],
        'completed': ['shipped', 'cancelled'],
        'shipped': ['delivered', 'cancelled'],
        'delivered': ['cancelled'],
        'cancelled': []
    }

    if new_status not in valid_transitions[current_status]:
        return

    if new_status == 'cancelled':
        for product_name, quantity in order['products'].items():
            if product_name in self.products:
                self.products[product_name]['available_stock'] += quantity
            else:
                price_snapshot = order['price_snapshot'].get(product_name, 0.0)
                self.products[product_name] = {
                    'price': price_snapshot,
                    'total_stock': quantity,
                    'available_stock': quantity
                }

    order['status'] = new_status

def get_system_state(self) -> dict:
    """Provides access to the current system state, including users, products, stock, carts, and orders."""
    return {
        "users": self.users,
        "products": {name: {"price": data["price"], "total_stock": data["total_stock"], "available_stock": data["available_stock"]}
                   for name, data in self.products.items()},
        "carts": self.carts,
        "reservations": self.reservations,
        "orders": self.orders,
        "next_order_sequence": self.next_order_sequence
    }

Regenerated code for EShopSystem.add_to_cart: 
def add_to_cart(self, user_id, product_name, quantity):
    # Check if user and product exist, and quantity is valid
    if (user_id not in self.users or
        product_name not in self.products or
        quantity <= 0):
        return

    # Check if product has sufficient available stock
    if self.products[product_name]['available_stock'] < quantity:
        return

    # Initialize cart and reservations if not present
    if user_id not in self.carts:
        self.carts[user_id] = {}
    if user_id not in self.reservations:
        self.reservations[user_id] = {}

    # Update cart quantity
    if product_name in self.carts[user_id]:
        self.carts[user_id][product_name] += quantity
    else:
        self.carts[user_id][product_name] = quantity

    # Update reservations and available stock
    if product_name in self.reservations[user_id]:
        self.reservations[user_id][product_name] += quantity
    else:
        self.reservations[user_id][product_name] = quantity

    self.products[product_name]['available_stock'] -= quantity

===== regenerate_single_method OUTPUT =====
Regenerated code for EShopSystem.remove_from_cart: 
def remove_from_cart(self, user_id: str, product_name: str, quantity: int) -> None:
    # Check if the product exists in the stock system
    product_exists_in_stock = product_name in self.products

    # Check if the user exists and has the product in their cart with sufficient quantity
    if (user_id not in self.users or
        product_name not in self.carts.get(user_id, {}) or
        quantity <= 0 or
        self.carts[user_id][product_name] < quantity):
        return

    # If the product no longer exists in the stock system, ignore the operation entirely
    if not product_exists_in_stock:
        return

    # Remove the reservation if it exists
    if (user_id in self.reservations and
        product_name in self.reservations[user_id] and
        self.reservations[user_id][product_name] >= quantity):
        if self.reservations[user_id][product_name] == quantity:
            del self.reservations[user_id][product_name]
        else:
            self.reservations[user_id][product_name] -= quantity

    # Reduce or remove the product from the cart
    if self.carts[user_id][product_name] == quantity:
        del self.carts[user_id][product_name]
    else:
        self.carts[user_id][product_name] -= quantity

    # Return the quantity to available stock only if product exists in stock system
    if product_exists_in_stock:
        self.products[product_name]['available_stock'] += quantity

===== regenerate_single_method OUTPUT =====
Regenerated code for EShopSystem.perform_checkout: 
def perform_checkout(self, user_id):
    # Check if user exists and has a non-empty cart
    if user_id not in self.users or user_id not in self.carts or not self.carts[user_id]:
        return None

    # Validate all reserved products exist in stock and have sufficient available quantity
    for product_name, quantity in self.carts[user_id].items():
        if product_name not in self.products or self.products[product_name]['available_stock'] < quantity:
            return None

    # Calculate order total using current prices
    order_total = 0.0
    price_snapshot = {}
    for product_name, quantity in self.carts[user_id].items():
        order_total += self.products[product_name]['price'] * quantity
        price_snapshot[product_name] = self.products[product_name]['price']

    # Generate order ID in thread-safe manner
    with threading.Lock():
        order_id = f"SK{self.next_order_sequence:05d}"
        self.next_order_sequence += 1

    # Create order with status 'pending'
    order_data = {
        'status': 'pending',
        'products': self.carts[user_id].copy(),
        'price_snapshot': price_snapshot,
        'total': order_total,
        'user_id': user_id
    }

    # Store cart, reservations, and stock state for potential rollback
    cart_backup = self.carts[user_id].copy()
    reservations_backup = self.reservations.get(user_id, {}).copy()
    stock_backup = {}
    for product_name, quantity in self.carts[user_id].items():
        stock_backup[product_name] = self.products[product_name]['available_stock']

    try:
        # Deduct stock from available_stock
        for product_name, quantity in self.carts[user_id].items():
            self.products[product_name]['available_stock'] -= quantity

        # Clear cart and reservations
        self.carts[user_id].clear()
        if user_id in self.reservations:
            self.reservations[user_id].clear()

        # Only after successful clearing, add the order
        self.orders[order_id] = order_data
        return order_id
    except:
        # Rollback changes if any error occurs
        self.carts[user_id] = cart_backup
        if user_id in self.reservations:
            self.reservations[user_id] = reservations_backup
        for product_name, quantity in stock_backup.items():
            self.products[product_name]['available_stock'] = quantity
        return None
===============================

def perform_checkout(self, user_id):
    # Check if user exists and has a non-empty cart
    if user_id not in self.users or user_id not in self.carts or not self.carts[user_id]:
        return None

    # Validate all reserved products exist in stock and have sufficient available quantity
    # Add validation that cart products match reservations
    if user_id in self.reservations:
        for product_name in self.reservations[user_id]:
            if product_name not in self.carts[user_id]:
                return None

    for product_name, quantity in self.carts[user_id].items():
        if product_name not in self.products or self.products[product_name]['available_stock'] < quantity:
            return None

    # Calculate order total using current prices
    order_total = 0.0
    price_snapshot = {}
    for product_name, quantity in self.carts[user_id].items():
        order_total += self.products[product_name]['price'] * quantity
        price_snapshot[product_name] = self.products[product_name]['price']

    # Generate order ID in thread-safe manner
    with threading.Lock():
        order_id = f"SK{self.next_order_sequence:05d}"
        self.next_order_sequence += 1

    # Create order with status 'pending'
    order_data = {
        'status': 'pending',
        'products': self.carts[user_id].copy(),
        'price_snapshot': price_snapshot,
        'total': order_total,
        'user_id': user_id
    }

    # Store cart, reservations, and stock state for potential rollback
    cart_backup = self.carts[user_id].copy()
    reservations_backup = self.reservations.get(user_id, {}).copy()
    stock_backup = {}
    for product_name, quantity in self.carts[user_id].items():
        stock_backup[product_name] = self.products[product_name]['available_stock']

    try:
        # Deduct stock from available_stock
        for product_name, quantity in self.carts[user_id].items():
            self.products[product_name]['available_stock'] -= quantity

        # Clear cart and reservations
        self.carts[user_id].clear()
        if user_id in self.reservations:
            self.reservations[user_id].clear()

        # Only after successful clearing, add the order
        self.orders[order_id] = order_data
        return order_id

        # Rollback changes if any error occurs
        self.carts[user_id] = cart_backup
        if user_id in self.reservations:
            self.reservations[user_id] = reservations_backup
        for product_name, quantity in stock_backup.items():
            self.products[product_name]['available_stock'] = quantity
        return None

===== assemble_program OUTPUT =====
import threading

class EShopSystem:
    def __init__(self):
        self.users = {}
        self.products = {}
        self.carts = {}
        self.reservations = {}
        self.orders = {}
        self.next_order_sequence = 1
        self.lock = threading.Lock()

    def create_user(self, user_id: str) -> None:
        if user_id not in self.users:
            self.users[user_id] = {}
            self.carts[user_id] = {}
            self.reservations[user_id] = {}

    def delete_user(self, user_id: str) -> None:
        if user_id not in self.users:
            return

        if user_id in self.reservations:
            for product_name, quantity in self.reservations[user_id].items():
                if product_name in self.products:
                    self.products[product_name]['available_stock'] += quantity
            del self.reservations[user_id]

        if user_id in self.carts:
            del self.carts[user_id]

        del self.users[user_id]

    def add_or_update_product(self, name: str, price: float, stock: int) -> None:
        if not name or price < 0 or stock < 0:
            return

        if name in self.products:
            product = self.products[name]
            product['price'] = price
            product['total_stock'] = stock
            product['available_stock'] = stock
        else:
            self.products[name] = {
                'price': price,
                'total_stock': stock,
                'available_stock': stock
            }

    def delete_product(self, name: str) -> None:
        if name in self.products:
            self.products[name]['available_stock'] = 0

    def add_to_cart(self, user_id: str, product_name: str, quantity: int) -> None:
        if (user_id not in self.users or
            product_name not in self.products or
            quantity <= 0):
            return

        if self.products[product_name]['available_stock'] < quantity:
            return

        if user_id not in self.carts:
            self.carts[user_id] = {}
        if user_id not in self.reservations:
            self.reservations[user_id] = {}

        if product_name in self.carts[user_id]:
            self.carts[user_id][product_name] += quantity
        else:
            self.carts[user_id][product_name] = quantity

        if product_name in self.reservations[user_id]:
            self.reservations[user_id][product_name] += quantity
        else:
            self.reservations[user_id][product_name] = quantity

        self.products[product_name]['available_stock'] -= quantity

    def remove_from_cart(self, user_id: str, product_name: str, quantity: int) -> None:
        product_exists_in_stock = product_name in self.products

        if (user_id not in self.users or
            product_name not in self.carts.get(user_id, {}) or
            quantity <= 0 or
            self.carts[user_id][product_name] < quantity):
            return

        if not product_exists_in_stock:
            return

        if (user_id in self.reservations and
            product_name in self.reservations[user_id] and
            self.reservations[user_id][product_name] >= quantity):
            if self.reservations[user_id][product_name] == quantity:
                del self.reservations[user_id][product_name]
            else:
                self.reservations[user_id][product_name] -= quantity

        if self.carts[user_id][product_name] == quantity:
            del self.carts[user_id][product_name]
        else:
            self.carts[user_id][product_name] -= quantity

        if product_exists_in_stock:
            self.products[product_name]['available_stock'] += quantity

    def perform_checkout(self, user_id: str) -> str:
        if user_id not in self.users or user_id not in self.carts or not self.carts[user_id]:
            return None

        if user_id in self.reservations:
            for product_name in self.reservations[user_id]:
                if product_name not in self.carts[user_id]:
                    return None

        for product_name, quantity in self.carts[user_id].items():
            if product_name not in self.products or self.products[product_name]['available_stock'] < quantity:
                return None

        order_total = 0.0
        price_snapshot = {}
        for product_name, quantity in self.carts[user_id].items():
            order_total += self.products[product_name]['price'] * quantity
            price_snapshot[product_name] = self.products[product_name]['price']

        with self.lock:
            order_id = f"SK{self.next_order_sequence:05d}"
            self.next_order_sequence += 1

        order_data = {
            'status': 'pending',
            'products': self.carts[user_id].copy(),
            'price_snapshot': price_snapshot,
            'total': order_total,
            'user_id': user_id
        }

        cart_backup = self.carts[user_id].copy()
        reservations_backup = self.reservations.get(user_id, {}).copy()
        stock_backup = {}
        for product_name, quantity in self.carts[user_id].items():
            stock_backup[product_name] = self.products[product_name]['available_stock']

        try:
            for product_name, quantity in self.carts[user_id].items():
                self.products[product_name]['available_stock'] -= quantity

            self.carts[user_id].clear()
            if user_id in self.reservations:
                self.reservations[user_id].clear()

            self.orders[order_id] = order_data
            return order_id
        except:
            self.carts[user_id] = cart_backup
            if user_id in self.reservations:
                self.reservations[user_id] = reservations_backup
            for product_name, quantity in stock_backup.items():
                self.products[product_name]['available_stock'] = quantity
            return None

    def update_order_status(self, order_id: str, new_status: str) -> None:
        if order_id not in self.orders:
            return

        order = self.orders[order_id]
        current_status = order['status']

        if current_status == 'cancelled':
            return

        valid_transitions = {
            'pending': ['processing', 'cancelled'],
            'processing': ['completed', 'cancelled'],
            'completed': ['shipped', 'cancelled'],
            'shipped': ['delivered', 'cancelled'],
            'delivered': ['cancelled'],
            'cancelled': []
        }

        if new_status not in valid_transitions[current_status]:
            return

        if new_status == 'cancelled':
            for product_name, quantity in order['products'].items():
                if product_name in self.products:
                    self.products[product_name]['available_stock'] += quantity
                else:
                    price_snapshot = order['price_snapshot'][product_name]
                    self.products[product_name] = {
                        'price': price_snapshot,
                        'total_stock': quantity,
                        'available_stock': quantity
                    }

        order['status'] = new_status

    def get_system_state(self) -> dict:
        return {
            "users": self.users,
            "products": {name: {"price": data["price"], "total_stock": data["total_stock"], "available_stock": data["available_stock"]}
                       for name, data in self.products.items()},
            "carts": self.carts,
            "reservations": self.reservations,
            "orders": self.orders,
            "next_order_sequence": self.next_order_sequence
        }

def main():
    system = EShopSystem()

    # Create users
    system.create_user("user1")
    system.create_user("user2")

    # Add products
    system.add_or_update_product("Laptop", 999.99, 10)
    system.add_or_update_product("Phone", 599.99, 20)

    # Add to cart
    system.add_to_cart("user1", "Laptop", 1)
    system.add_to_cart("user1", "Phone", 2)

    # Perform checkout
    order_id = system.perform_checkout("user1")
    print(f"Order created with ID: {order_id}")

    # Update order status
    system.update_order_status(order_id, "processing")
    system.update_order_status(order_id, "completed")

    # Get system state
    state = system.get_system_state()
    print("System state:", state)

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
