===== USE_CASE_EXTRACTION_FAILED OUTPUT =====
Here's the structured Use Case derived from the provided program:

---

**Use Case: Manage E-Shop Operations**

**Primary Actor:** User (Customer or Administrator)

**Goal:**
To perform essential e-commerce operations including user management, product management, shopping cart operations, order processing, and order status updates within an e-shop system.

---

**System Responsibilities:**
1. **User Management**
   - Create new user accounts.
   - Delete existing user accounts and release associated resources (cart, reservations, stock).

2. **Product Management**
   - Add new products or update existing product details (price, stock).
   - Delete products by marking them as unavailable (stock = 0).

3. **Shopping Cart Management**
   - Add products to a user’s shopping cart with stock reservation.
   - Remove products from a user’s shopping cart with stock release.
   - Ensure stock availability before adding products to cart.

4. **Order Processing**
   - Perform checkout to create an order from a user’s cart.
   - Generate unique order IDs with sequential numbering.
   - Calculate order total based on current product prices.
   - Reserve stock for ordered products and clear the cart upon successful checkout.
   - Support atomic checkout operations with rollback on failure.

5. **Order Status Management**
   - Update order status following predefined state transitions (e.g., pending → processing → completed → shipped → delivered).
   - Cancel orders and release reserved stock back to inventory.
   - Handle stock restoration for cancelled orders, including products no longer in inventory.

6. **System State Monitoring**
   - Provide a snapshot of the current system state, including users, products, carts, reservations, and orders.

---

**Preconditions:**
1. The e-shop system is initialized and operational.
2. For user-specific operations (e.g., add to cart, checkout), the user must exist in the system.
3. For product-specific operations (e.g., add to cart), the product must exist in the system with sufficient stock.
4. For order status updates, the order must exist in the system.

---

**Postconditions:**
1. **User Management**
   - A new user is created with an empty cart and reservation list (if `create_user` succeeds).
   - A user is removed from the system, and all associated cart items, reservations, and stock are released (if `delete_user` succeeds).

2. **Product Management**
   - A product is added or updated with valid price and stock (if `add_or_update_product` succeeds).
   - A product is marked as unavailable (stock = 0) (if `delete_product` succeeds).

3. **Shopping Cart Management**
   - A product is added to the user’s cart, and stock is reserved (if `add_to_cart` succeeds).
   - A product is removed from the user’s cart, and stock is released (if `remove_from_cart` succeeds).

4. **Order Processing**
   - An order is created with a unique ID, and the user’s cart is cleared (if `perform_checkout` succeeds).
   - Stock is reserved for all ordered products, and the order is recorded in the system.

5. **Order Status Management**
   - The order status is updated, and stock is released if the order is cancelled (if `update_order_status` succeeds).

6. **System State Monitoring**
   - A snapshot of the current system state is provided (if `get_system_state` is called).

---

**Main Success Scenario:**

1. **Create User**
   - *Actor:* Administrator
   - *System:* Validates the user does not already exist.
   - *System:* Creates a new user account with an empty cart and reservation list.

2. **Add/Update Product**
   - *Actor:* Administrator
   - *System:* Validates the product name, price (≥ 0), and stock (≥ 0).
   - *System:* Adds a new product or updates an existing product with the provided details.

3. **Add Product to Cart**
   - *Actor:* User
   - *System:* Validates the user and product exist, and the requested quantity is available.
   - *System:* Adds the product to the user’s cart and reserves the stock.

4. **Remove Product from Cart**
   - *Actor:* User
   - *System:* Validates the user, product, and quantity to remove.
   - *System:* Removes the product from the cart and releases the reserved stock.

5. **Perform Checkout**
   - *Actor:* User
   - *System:* Validates the user’s cart is not empty and all products are available.
   - *System:* Calculates the order total and creates a unique order ID.
   - *System:* Reserves stock for all ordered products, clears the cart, and records the order.
   - *System:* Returns the order ID to the user.

6. **Update Order Status**
   - *Actor:* Administrator
   - *System:* Validates the order exists and the new status is a valid transition.
   - *System:* Updates the order status and releases stock if the order is cancelled.

7. **View System State**
   - *Actor:* Administrator
   - *System:* Returns a snapshot of the current system state, including users, products, carts, reservations, and orders.

---

**Extensions:**

1. **Create User**
   - *1a. User already exists*
     - *System:* Ignores the request and takes no action.

2. **Add/Update Product**
   - *2a. Invalid product details (e.g., negative price or stock)*
     - *System:* Ignores the request and takes no action.

3. **Add Product to Cart**
   - *3a. User or product does not exist*
     - *System:* Ignores the request and takes no action.
   - *3b. Insufficient stock*
     - *System:* Ignores the request and takes no action.

4. **Remove Product from Cart**
   - *4a. User, product, or quantity is invalid*
     - *System:* Ignores the request and takes no action.
   - *4b. Product no longer exists in inventory*
     - *System:* Removes the product from the cart but does not release stock.

5. **Perform Checkout**
   - *5a. User’s cart is empty or invalid*
     - *System:* Returns `None` and takes no action.
   - *5b. Insufficient stock for one or more products*
     - *System:* Returns `None` and takes no action.
   - *5c. Checkout fails (e.g., system error)*
     - *System:* Rolls back all changes (cart, reservations, stock) and returns `None`.

6. **Update Order Status**
   - *6a. Order does not exist*
     - *System:* Ignores the request and takes no action.
   - *6b. Invalid status transition (e.g., completed → processing)*
     - *System:* Ignores the request and takes no action.
   - *6c. Order is already cancelled*
     - *System:* Ignores the request and takes no action.

7. **Delete User**
   - *7a. User does not exist*
     - *System:* Ignores the request and takes no action.

8. **Delete Product**
   - *8a. Product does not exist*
     - *System:* Ignores the request and takes no action.

---

**Special Requirements:**
1. **Thread Safety**
   - The system must support concurrent operations (e.g., multiple users adding to cart or checking out simultaneously) without data corruption.
   - Order ID generation must be thread-safe to ensure uniqueness.

2. **Atomic Operations**
   - Checkout operations must be atomic: either all steps (stock reservation, cart clearing, order creation) succeed, or none do.

3. **Stock Consistency**
   - The system must ensure stock availability is accurately reflected in real-time to prevent overselling.

4. **Order State Transitions**
   - Order status updates must follow predefined transitions to maintain consistency (e.g., an order cannot transition from "completed" to "processing").

---

===== assemble_program OUTPUT =====
import threading

class EShopSystem:
    def __init__(self):
        self.users = {}
        self.products = {}
        self.carts = {}
        self.reservations = {}
        self.orders = {}
        self.next_order_sequence = 1
        self.lock = threading.Lock()

    def create_user(self, user_id: str) -> None:
        if user_id not in self.users:
            self.users[user_id] = {}
            self.carts[user_id] = {}
            self.reservations[user_id] = {}

    def delete_user(self, user_id: str) -> None:
        if user_id not in self.users:
            return

        if user_id in self.reservations:
            for product_name, quantity in self.reservations[user_id].items():
                if product_name in self.products:
                    self.products[product_name]['available_stock'] += quantity
            del self.reservations[user_id]

        if user_id in self.carts:
            del self.carts[user_id]

        del self.users[user_id]

    def add_or_update_product(self, name: str, price: float, stock: int) -> None:
        if not name or price < 0 or stock < 0:
            return

        if name in self.products:
            product = self.products[name]
            product['price'] = price
            product['total_stock'] = stock
            product['available_stock'] = stock
        else:
            self.products[name] = {
                'price': price,
                'total_stock': stock,
                'available_stock': stock
            }

    def delete_product(self, name: str) -> None:
        if name in self.products:
            del self.products[name]

    def add_to_cart(self, user_id: str, product_name: str, quantity: int) -> None:
        if (user_id not in self.users or
            product_name not in self.products or
            quantity <= 0):
            return

        if self.products[product_name]['available_stock'] < quantity:
            return

        if user_id not in self.carts:
            self.carts[user_id] = {}
        if user_id not in self.reservations:
            self.reservations[user_id] = {}

        if product_name in self.carts[user_id]:
            self.carts[user_id][product_name] += quantity
        else:
            self.carts[user_id][product_name] = quantity

        if product_name in self.reservations[user_id]:
            self.reservations[user_id][product_name] += quantity
        else:
            self.reservations[user_id][product_name] = quantity

        self.products[product_name]['available_stock'] -= quantity

    def remove_from_cart(self, user_id: str, product_name: str, quantity: int) -> None:
        product_exists_in_stock = product_name in self.products

        if (user_id not in self.users or
            product_name not in self.carts.get(user_id, {}) or
            quantity <= 0 or
            self.carts[user_id][product_name] < quantity):
            return

        if (user_id in self.reservations and
            product_name in self.reservations[user_id] and
            self.reservations[user_id][product_name] >= quantity):
            if self.reservations[user_id][product_name] == quantity:
                del self.reservations[user_id][product_name]
            else:
                self.reservations[user_id][product_name] -= quantity

        if self.carts[user_id][product_name] == quantity:
            del self.carts[user_id][product_name]
        else:
            self.carts[user_id][product_name] -= quantity

        if product_exists_in_stock:
            self.products[product_name]['available_stock'] += quantity

    def perform_checkout(self, user_id: str) -> str:
        if user_id not in self.users or user_id not in self.carts or not self.carts[user_id]:
            return None

        if user_id in self.reservations:
            for product_name in self.reservations[user_id]:
                if product_name not in self.carts[user_id]:
                    return None

        order_total = 0.0
        price_snapshot = {}
        for product_name, quantity in self.carts[user_id].items():
            order_total += self.products[product_name]['price'] * quantity
            price_snapshot[product_name] = self.products[product_name]['price']

        with self.lock:
            order_id = f"SK{self.next_order_sequence:05d}"
            self.next_order_sequence += 1

        order_data = {
            'status': 'pending',
            'products': self.carts[user_id].copy(),
            'price_snapshot': price_snapshot,
            'total': order_total,
            'user_id': user_id
        }

        cart_backup = self.carts[user_id].copy()
        reservations_backup = self.reservations.get(user_id, {}).copy()

        try:
            self.carts[user_id].clear()
            if user_id in self.reservations:
                self.reservations[user_id].clear()

            self.orders[order_id] = order_data
            return order_id
        except:
            self.carts[user_id] = cart_backup
            if user_id in self.reservations:
                self.reservations[user_id] = reservations_backup
            return None

    def update_order_status(self, order_id: str, new_status: str) -> None:
        if order_id not in self.orders:
            return

        order = self.orders[order_id]
        current_status = order['status']

        if current_status == 'cancelled':
            return

        valid_transitions = {
            'pending': ['processing', 'cancelled'],
            'processing': ['completed', 'cancelled'],
            'completed': ['shipped', 'cancelled'],
            'shipped': ['delivered', 'cancelled'],
            'delivered': ['cancelled'],
            'cancelled': []
        }

        if new_status not in valid_transitions[current_status]:
            return

        if new_status == 'cancelled':
            for product_name, quantity in order['products'].items():
                if product_name in self.products:
                    self.products[product_name]['available_stock'] += quantity
                else:
                    price_snapshot = order['price_snapshot'][product_name]
                    self.products[product_name] = {
                        'price': price_snapshot,
                        'total_stock': quantity,
                        'available_stock': quantity
                    }

        order['status'] = new_status

    def get_system_state(self) -> dict:
        return {
            "users": self.users,
            "products": {name: {"price": data["price"], "total_stock": data["total_stock"], "available_stock": data["available_stock"]}
                       for name, data in self.products.items()},
            "carts": self.carts,
            "reservations": self.reservations,
            "orders": self.orders,
            "next_order_sequence": self.next_order_sequence
        }

def main():
    system = EShopSystem()

    # Create users
    system.create_user("user1")
    system.create_user("user2")

    # Add products
    system.add_or_update_product("Laptop", 999.99, 10)
    system.add_or_update_product("Phone", 599.99, 20)

    # Add to cart
    system.add_to_cart("user1", "Laptop", 1)
    system.add_to_cart("user1", "Phone", 2)

    # Perform checkout
    order_id = system.perform_checkout("user1")
    print(f"Order created with ID: {order_id}")

    # Update order status
    system.update_order_status(order_id, "processing")
    system.update_order_status(order_id, "completed")

    # Get system state
    state = system.get_system_state()
    print("System state:", state)

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

===== USE_CASE_EXTRACTION_FAILED OUTPUT =====
Here's the structured Use Case derived from the provided program:

---

**Use Case: Manage E-Shop Operations**

**Primary Actor:** User (Customer, Administrator)

**Goal:**
To enable users to manage accounts, browse and purchase products, and allow administrators to manage product inventory and order statuses within an e-shop system.

---

### **System Responsibilities:**
1. **User Management**
   - Create and delete user accounts.
   - Maintain user-specific shopping carts and product reservations.

2. **Product Management**
   - Add, update, or remove products from the inventory.
   - Track product stock levels (total and available).
   - Adjust available stock when products are reserved or released.

3. **Shopping Cart Management**
   - Allow users to add or remove products from their carts.
   - Reserve products in stock when added to a cart.
   - Release reserved products when removed from a cart or when an order is cancelled.

4. **Order Processing**
   - Generate unique order IDs for successful checkouts.
   - Calculate order totals based on current product prices.
   - Maintain a snapshot of product prices at the time of checkout.
   - Support order status transitions (e.g., pending → processing → completed → shipped → delivered → cancelled).
   - Restore product stock if an order is cancelled.

5. **Concurrency Control**
   - Ensure thread-safe operations for order creation and stock management.

6. **System State Monitoring**
   - Provide a snapshot of the system state, including users, products, carts, reservations, and orders.

---

### **Preconditions:**
1. The e-shop system is initialized and operational.
2. For user-specific actions (e.g., adding to cart, checkout), the user must exist in the system.
3. For product-specific actions (e.g., adding to cart), the product must exist in the inventory.
4. For order status updates, the order must exist in the system.

---

### **Postconditions:**
1. **User Management**
   - A new user account is created with an associated empty cart and reservation list.
   - A deleted user account is removed, along with their cart, reservations, and any unreleased stock.

2. **Product Management**
   - A new product is added to the inventory with specified price and stock levels.
   - An existing product’s price and stock levels are updated.
   - A deleted product is removed from the inventory.

3. **Shopping Cart Management**
   - Products added to a cart are reserved in stock and reflected in the user’s cart.
   - Products removed from a cart have their stock released and are removed from the cart.

4. **Order Processing**
   - A successful checkout generates an order with a unique ID, pending status, and a snapshot of product prices.
   - The user’s cart and reservations are cleared upon successful checkout.
   - Order status transitions update the order’s state and adjust product stock if cancelled.

5. **System State**
   - The system state reflects all changes made during operations (e.g., user creation, product updates, order status changes).

---

### **Main Success Scenario:**

#### **1. Create User Account**
1. User requests to create an account with a unique user ID.
2. System creates the user account, initializes an empty cart, and sets up an empty reservation list.

#### **2. Add/Update Product (Administrator)**
1. Administrator provides product details (name, price, stock).
2. System validates the input (non-empty name, non-negative price/stock).
3. System adds the product to the inventory or updates existing product details.

#### **3. Add Product to Cart**
1. User selects a product and specifies a quantity.
2. System validates the user, product, and quantity (must be positive and ≤ available stock).
3. System reserves the product quantity in stock and adds it to the user’s cart.

#### **4. Remove Product from Cart**
1. User selects a product in their cart and specifies a quantity to remove.
2. System validates the user, product, and quantity (must be ≤ cart quantity).
3. System releases the reserved stock and updates the cart.

#### **5. Perform Checkout**
1. User requests to checkout their cart.
2. System validates the user’s cart is non-empty and all reserved products are still in the cart.
3. System calculates the order total and creates a price snapshot.
4. System generates a unique order ID and creates an order with "pending" status.
5. System clears the user’s cart and reservations.
6. System returns the order ID to the user.

#### **6. Update Order Status (Administrator)**
1. Administrator provides an order ID and a new status.
2. System validates the order exists and the status transition is valid.
3. System updates the order status.
4. If the new status is "cancelled," system restores the product stock.

#### **7. Delete User Account**
1. User requests to delete their account.
2. System releases all reserved stock for the user.
3. System removes the user’s cart, reservations, and account.

#### **8. Delete Product (Administrator)**
1. Administrator requests to delete a product.
2. System removes the product from the inventory.

---

### **Extensions:**

#### **1a. User Account Creation Fails**
- If the user ID already exists, the system ignores the request.

#### **2a. Product Addition/Update Fails**
- If the product details are invalid (e.g., negative price/stock), the system ignores the request.

#### **3a. Add to Cart Fails**
- If the user, product, or quantity is invalid, or stock is insufficient, the system ignores the request.

#### **4a. Remove from Cart Fails**
- If the user, product, or quantity is invalid, or the product is not in the cart, the system ignores the request.

#### **5a. Checkout Fails**
- If the user’s cart is empty or reserved products are missing, the system returns `None` (no order ID).

#### **6a. Order Status Update Fails**
- If the order does not exist, the status transition is invalid, or the order is already cancelled, the system ignores the request.

#### **7a. User Deletion Fails**
- If the user does not exist, the system ignores the request.

#### **8a. Product Deletion Fails**
- If the product does not exist, the system ignores the request.

---

### **Special Requirements:**
1. **Thread Safety:**
   - The system must ensure thread-safe operations for order creation and stock management to prevent race conditions.

2. **Data Consistency:**
   - The system must maintain consistency between carts, reservations, and product stock levels during concurrent operations.

3. **Order ID Format:**
   - Order IDs must follow the format `SKXXXXX`, where `XXXXX` is a zero-padded sequence number.

4. **Stock Management:**
   - The system must restore product stock if an order is cancelled or a user is deleted.

---

===== assemble_program OUTPUT =====
import threading

class EShopSystem:
    def __init__(self):
        self.users = {}
        self.products = {}
        self.carts = {}
        self.reservations = {}
        self.orders = {}
        self.next_order_sequence = 1
        self.lock = threading.Lock()

    def create_user(self, user_id: str) -> None:
        if user_id not in self.users:
            self.users[user_id] = {}
            self.carts[user_id] = {}
            self.reservations[user_id] = {}

    def delete_user(self, user_id: str) -> None:
        if user_id not in self.users:
            return

        if user_id in self.reservations:
            for product_name, quantity in self.reservations[user_id].items():
                if product_name in self.products:
                    self.products[product_name]['available_stock'] += quantity
            del self.reservations[user_id]

        if user_id in self.carts:
            del self.carts[user_id]

        del self.users[user_id]

    def add_or_update_product(self, name: str, price: float, stock: int) -> None:
        if not name or price < 0 or stock < 0:
            return

        if name in self.products:
            product = self.products[name]
            product['price'] = price
            product['total_stock'] = stock
            product['available_stock'] = stock
        else:
            self.products[name] = {
                'price': price,
                'total_stock': stock,
                'available_stock': stock
            }

    def delete_product(self, name: str) -> None:
        if name in self.products:
            del self.products[name]

    def add_to_cart(self, user_id: str, product_name: str, quantity: int) -> None:
        if (user_id not in self.users or
            product_name not in self.products or
            quantity <= 0):
            return

        if self.products[product_name]['available_stock'] < quantity:
            return

        if user_id not in self.carts:
            self.carts[user_id] = {}
        if user_id not in self.reservations:
            self.reservations[user_id] = {}

        if product_name in self.carts[user_id]:
            self.carts[user_id][product_name] += quantity
        else:
            self.carts[user_id][product_name] = quantity

        if product_name in self.reservations[user_id]:
            self.reservations[user_id][product_name] += quantity
        else:
            self.reservations[user_id][product_name] = quantity

        self.products[product_name]['available_stock'] -= quantity

    def remove_from_cart(self, user_id: str, product_name: str, quantity: int) -> None:
        product_exists_in_stock = product_name in self.products

        if (user_id not in self.users or
            product_name not in self.carts.get(user_id, {}) or
            quantity <= 0 or
            self.carts[user_id][product_name] < quantity):
            return

        if (user_id in self.reservations and
            product_name in self.reservations[user_id] and
            self.reservations[user_id][product_name] >= quantity):
            if self.reservations[user_id][product_name] == quantity:
                del self.reservations[user_id][product_name]
            else:
                self.reservations[user_id][product_name] -= quantity

        if self.carts[user_id][product_name] == quantity:
            del self.carts[user_id][product_name]
        else:
            self.carts[user_id][product_name] -= quantity

        if product_exists_in_stock:
            self.products[product_name]['available_stock'] += quantity

    def perform_checkout(self, user_id: str) -> str:
        if user_id not in self.users or user_id not in self.carts or not self.carts[user_id]:
            return None

        # Validate all reserved products exist in stock
        for product_name in self.carts[user_id]:
            if product_name not in self.products:
                return None

        order_total = 0.0
        price_snapshot = {}
        for product_name, quantity in self.carts[user_id].items():
            order_total += self.products[product_name]['price'] * quantity
            price_snapshot[product_name] = self.products[product_name]['price']

        with self.lock:
            order_id = f"SK{self.next_order_sequence:05d}"
            self.next_order_sequence += 1

            order_data = {
                'status': 'pending',
                'products': self.carts[user_id].copy(),
                'price_snapshot': price_snapshot,
                'total': order_total,
                'user_id': user_id
            }

            cart_backup = self.carts[user_id].copy()
            reservations_backup = self.reservations.get(user_id, {}).copy()

            try:
                self.carts[user_id].clear()
                if user_id in self.reservations:
                    self.reservations[user_id].clear()

                self.orders[order_id] = order_data
                return order_id
            except:
                self.carts[user_id] = cart_backup
                if user_id in self.reservations:
                    self.reservations[user_id] = reservations_backup
                return None

    def update_order_status(self, order_id: str, new_status: str) -> None:
        if order_id not in self.orders:
            return

        order = self.orders[order_id]
        current_status = order['status']

        if current_status == 'cancelled':
            return

        valid_transitions = {
            'pending': ['processing', 'cancelled'],
            'processing': ['completed', 'cancelled'],
            'completed': ['shipped', 'cancelled'],
            'shipped': ['delivered', 'cancelled'],
            'delivered': ['cancelled'],
            'cancelled': []
        }

        if new_status not in valid_transitions[current_status]:
            return

        if new_status == 'cancelled':
            for product_name, quantity in order['products'].items():
                if product_name in self.products:
                    self.products[product_name]['available_stock'] += quantity
                else:
                    price_snapshot = order['price_snapshot'][product_name]
                    self.products[product_name] = {
                        'price': price_snapshot,
                        'total_stock': quantity,
                        'available_stock': quantity
                    }

        order['status'] = new_status

    def get_system_state(self) -> dict:
        return {
            "users": self.users,
            "products": {name: {"price": data["price"], "total_stock": data["total_stock"], "available_stock": data["available_stock"]}
                       for name, data in self.products.items()},
            "carts": self.carts,
            "reservations": self.reservations,
            "orders": self.orders,
            "next_order_sequence": self.next_order_sequence
        }

def main():
    system = EShopSystem()

    # Create users
    system.create_user("user1")
    system.create_user("user2")

    # Add products
    system.add_or_update_product("Laptop", 999.99, 10)
    system.add_or_update_product("Phone", 599.99, 20)

    # Add to cart
    system.add_to_cart("user1", "Laptop", 1)
    system.add_to_cart("user1", "Phone", 2)

    # Perform checkout
    order_id = system.perform_checkout("user1")
    print(f"Order created with ID: {order_id}")

    # Update order status
    system.update_order_status(order_id, "processing")
    system.update_order_status(order_id, "completed")

    # Get system state
    state = system.get_system_state()
    print("System state:", state)

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

===== USE_CASE_EXTRACTION_FAILED OUTPUT =====
Here's the structured Use Case derived from the provided program:

---

**Use Case: Manage E-Shop Operations**

**Primary Actor:** Customer, Administrator

**Goal:**
- Customer: Purchase products through the e-shop system
- Administrator: Manage users, products, and orders in the e-shop system

---

**System Responsibilities:**
1. **User Management**
   - Create and delete user accounts
   - Maintain user-specific shopping carts and product reservations

2. **Product Management**
   - Add, update, and delete products with pricing and stock information
   - Track total and available stock for each product

3. **Shopping Cart Management**
   - Allow users to add/remove products to/from their cart
   - Reserve product stock when items are added to cart
   - Release reserved stock when items are removed from cart or orders are cancelled

4. **Order Processing**
   - Process checkout to create orders from cart contents
   - Generate unique order IDs with sequential numbering
   - Maintain order status with valid state transitions
   - Update product stock when orders are cancelled

5. **State Management**
   - Provide thread-safe operations for concurrent access
   - Maintain consistent system state across all operations
   - Allow retrieval of current system state for monitoring

---

**Preconditions:**
1. System is initialized with empty user, product, cart, reservation, and order collections
2. Next order sequence number is set to 1

---

**Postconditions:**
1. **User Management**
   - New user accounts are created with empty carts and reservations
   - Deleted user accounts are removed along with their carts and reservations
   - Reserved stock is released when users are deleted

2. **Product Management**
   - New products are added with specified price and stock
   - Existing products are updated with new price and stock values
   - Deleted products are removed from the system

3. **Shopping Cart Management**
   - Products are added to user carts with stock reserved
   - Products are removed from user carts with stock released
   - Cart operations fail if user or product doesn't exist or quantity is invalid

4. **Order Processing**
   - Successful checkout creates an order with "pending" status
   - Order contains product list, price snapshot, total amount, and user reference
   - Cart is cleared after successful checkout
   - Order status can be updated through valid transitions
   - Cancelled orders release reserved stock back to available inventory

---

**Main Success Scenario:**

1. **User Registration**
   - Administrator requests to create a new user account
   - System creates user account with empty cart and reservation collections

2. **Product Setup**
   - Administrator adds a new product with name, price, and stock quantity
   - System stores product information with available stock equal to total stock

3. **Shopping Process**
   - Customer requests to add product to cart with specified quantity
   - System verifies product exists and has sufficient stock
   - System adds product to customer's cart and reserves the stock
   - Customer requests to remove product from cart
   - System removes product from cart and releases the reserved stock

4. **Checkout Process**
   - Customer requests to checkout their cart
   - System verifies all cart items exist in stock
   - System calculates order total based on current prices
   - System creates new order with unique ID, "pending" status, and price snapshot
   - System clears customer's cart and reservations
   - System returns order ID to customer

5. **Order Fulfillment**
   - Administrator updates order status to "processing"
   - System changes order status to "processing"
   - Administrator updates order status to "completed"
   - System changes order status to "completed"

---

**Extensions:**

1. **User Management**
   - *1a. User already exists*
     - System ignores create user request
   - *1b. User doesn't exist for deletion*
     - System ignores delete user request

2. **Product Management**
   - *2a. Invalid product data (empty name, negative price/stock)*
     - System ignores add/update product request
   - *2b. Product doesn't exist for deletion*
     - System ignores delete product request

3. **Shopping Cart Management**
   - *3a. User doesn't exist*
     - System ignores add/remove cart item request
   - *3b. Product doesn't exist*
     - System ignores add/remove cart item request
   - *3c. Invalid quantity (≤ 0 or exceeds available stock)*
     - System ignores add cart item request
   - *3d. Insufficient quantity in cart for removal*
     - System ignores remove cart item request

4. **Checkout Process**
   - *4a. User doesn't exist or cart is empty*
     - System returns null (no order created)
   - *4b. Product in cart no longer exists*
     - System returns null (no order created)
   - *4c. Checkout fails during processing*
     - System restores cart and reservations to previous state
     - System returns null (no order created)

5. **Order Status Update**
   - *5a. Order doesn't exist*
     - System ignores status update request
   - *5b. Invalid status transition*
     - System ignores status update request
   - *5c. Order is already cancelled*
     - System ignores status update request
   - *5d. Product no longer exists when cancelling order*
     - System recreates product with original price and released stock

---

**Special Requirements:**
1. The system must support concurrent access from multiple users through thread-safe operations
2. Order IDs must be generated in sequential format (SK00001, SK00002, etc.)
3. All stock reservations must be properly released when:
   - Products are removed from cart
   - Users are deleted
   - Orders are cancelled
4. Price snapshots must be taken at checkout to preserve pricing for historical orders

---

===== assemble_program OUTPUT =====
import threading

class EShopSystem:
    def __init__(self):
        self.users = {}
        self.products = {}
        self.carts = {}
        self.reservations = {}
        self.orders = {}
        self.next_order_sequence = 1
        self.lock = threading.Lock()

    def create_user(self, user_id: str) -> None:
        if user_id not in self.users:
            self.users[user_id] = {}
            self.carts[user_id] = {}
            self.reservations[user_id] = {}

    def delete_user(self, user_id: str) -> None:
        if user_id not in self.users:
            return

        if user_id in self.reservations:
            for product_name, quantity in self.reservations[user_id].items():
                if product_name in self.products:
                    self.products[product_name]['available_stock'] += quantity
            del self.reservations[user_id]

        if user_id in self.carts:
            del self.carts[user_id]

        del self.users[user_id]

    def add_or_update_product(self, name: str, price: float, stock: int) -> None:
        if not name or price < 0 or stock < 0:
            return

        if name in self.products:
            product = self.products[name]
            product['price'] = price
            product['total_stock'] = stock
            product['available_stock'] = stock
        else:
            self.products[name] = {
                'price': price,
                'total_stock': stock,
                'available_stock': stock
            }

    def delete_product(self, name: str) -> None:
        if name in self.products:
            del self.products[name]

    def add_to_cart(self, user_id: str, product_name: str, quantity: int) -> None:
        if (user_id not in self.users or
            product_name not in self.products or
            quantity <= 0):
            return

        if self.products[product_name]['available_stock'] < quantity:
            return

        if user_id not in self.carts:
            self.carts[user_id] = {}
        if user_id not in self.reservations:
            self.reservations[user_id] = {}

        if product_name in self.carts[user_id]:
            self.carts[user_id][product_name] += quantity
        else:
            self.carts[user_id][product_name] = quantity

        if product_name in self.reservations[user_id]:
            self.reservations[user_id][product_name] += quantity
        else:
            self.reservations[user_id][product_name] = quantity

        self.products[product_name]['available_stock'] -= quantity

    def remove_from_cart(self, user_id: str, product_name: str, quantity: int) -> None:
        product_exists_in_stock = product_name in self.products

        if (user_id not in self.users or
            product_name not in self.carts.get(user_id, {}) or
            quantity <= 0 or
            self.carts[user_id][product_name] < quantity):
            return

        if (user_id in self.reservations and
            product_name in self.reservations[user_id] and
            self.reservations[user_id][product_name] >= quantity):
            if self.reservations[user_id][product_name] == quantity:
                del self.reservations[user_id][product_name]
            else:
                self.reservations[user_id][product_name] -= quantity

        if self.carts[user_id][product_name] == quantity:
            del self.carts[user_id][product_name]
        else:
            self.carts[user_id][product_name] -= quantity

        if product_exists_in_stock:
            self.products[product_name]['available_stock'] += quantity

    def perform_checkout(self, user_id: str) -> str:
        if user_id not in self.users or user_id not in self.carts or not self.carts[user_id]:
            return None

        # Validate all reserved products exist in stock
        for product_name in self.carts[user_id]:
            if product_name not in self.products:
                return None

        order_total = 0.0
        price_snapshot = {}
        for product_name, quantity in self.carts[user_id].items():
            order_total += self.products[product_name]['price'] * quantity
            price_snapshot[product_name] = self.products[product_name]['price']

        with self.lock:
            order_id = f"SK{self.next_order_sequence:05d}"
            self.next_order_sequence += 1

            order_data = {
                'status': 'pending',
                'products': self.carts[user_id].copy(),
                'price_snapshot': price_snapshot,
                'total': order_total,
                'user_id': user_id
            }

            cart_backup = self.carts[user_id].copy()
            reservations_backup = self.reservations.get(user_id, {}).copy()

            try:
                self.carts[user_id].clear()
                if user_id in self.reservations:
                    self.reservations[user_id].clear()

                self.orders[order_id] = order_data
                return order_id
            except:
                self.carts[user_id] = cart_backup
                if user_id in self.reservations:
                    self.reservations[user_id] = reservations_backup
                return None

    def update_order_status(self, order_id: str, new_status: str) -> None:
        if order_id not in self.orders:
            return

        order = self.orders[order_id]
        current_status = order['status']

        if current_status == 'cancelled':
            return

        valid_transitions = {
            'pending': ['processing', 'cancelled'],
            'processing': ['completed', 'cancelled'],
            'completed': ['shipped', 'cancelled'],
            'shipped': ['delivered', 'cancelled'],
            'delivered': ['cancelled'],
            'cancelled': []
        }

        if new_status not in valid_transitions[current_status]:
            return

        if new_status == 'cancelled':
            for product_name, quantity in order['products'].items():
                if product_name in self.products:
                    self.products[product_name]['available_stock'] += quantity
                else:
                    price_snapshot = order['price_snapshot'][product_name]
                    self.products[product_name] = {
                        'price': price_snapshot,
                        'total_stock': quantity,
                        'available_stock': quantity
                    }

        order['status'] = new_status

    def get_system_state(self) -> dict:
        return {
            "users": self.users,
            "products": {name: {"price": data["price"], "total_stock": data["total_stock"], "available_stock": data["available_stock"]}
                       for name, data in self.products.items()},
            "carts": self.carts,
            "reservations": self.reservations,
            "orders": self.orders,
            "next_order_sequence": self.next_order_sequence
        }

def main():
    system = EShopSystem()

    # Create users
    system.create_user("user1")
    system.create_user("user2")

    # Add products
    system.add_or_update_product("Laptop", 999.99, 10)
    system.add_or_update_product("Phone", 599.99, 20)

    # Add to cart
    system.add_to_cart("user1", "Laptop", 1)
    system.add_to_cart("user1", "Phone", 2)

    # Perform checkout
    order_id = system.perform_checkout("user1")
    print(f"Order created with ID: {order_id}")

    # Update order status
    system.update_order_status(order_id, "processing")
    system.update_order_status(order_id, "completed")

    # Get system state
    state = system.get_system_state()
    print("System state:", state)

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

def test_main_success_scenario():
    system = EShopSystem()

    # Create user
    system.create_user("user1")

    # Add product
    system.add_or_update_product("laptop", 999.99, 10)

    # Add to cart
    system.add_to_cart("user1", "laptop", 2)

    # Perform checkout
    order_id = system.perform_checkout("user1")

    # Verify order was created
    assert order_id == "SK00001"
    assert system.orders[order_id]["status"] == "pending"
    assert system.orders[order_id]["products"]["laptop"] == 2

def test_product_cancellation_scenario():
    system = EShopSystem()

    # Create user and add product
    system.create_user("user2")
    system.add_or_update_product("phone", 599.99, 5)

    # Add to cart and checkout
    system.add_to_cart("user2", "phone", 3)
    order_id = system.perform_checkout("user2")

    # Cancel the order
    system.update_order_status(order_id, "cancelled")

    # Verify cancellation
    assert system.orders[order_id]["status"] == "cancelled"
    assert system.products["phone"]["available_stock"] == 5  # Stock should be restored

def test_edge_case_stock_management():
    system = EShopSystem()

    # Create user and add product with zero stock
    system.create_user("user3")
    system.add_or_update_product("tablet", 299.99, 0)

    # Try to add to cart (should fail)
    system.add_to_cart("user3", "tablet", 1)

    # Verify cart is empty
    assert "tablet" not in system.carts["user3"]

    # Update product stock
    system.add_or_update_product("tablet", 299.99, 5)

    # Now add to cart should succeed
    system.add_to_cart("user3", "tablet", 2)
    assert system.carts["user3"]["tablet"] == 2
    assert system.products["tablet"]["available_stock"] == 3

===== OUTPUT_review_activity_diagram OUTPUT =====
Here are the identified defects in the activity diagram:

- Missing atomicity handling in checkout flow: diagram shows sequential steps after order creation without indicating that cart clearing and reservation clearing must occur atomically (as implemented in the `try-except` block in `perform_checkout` method)
- Incorrect order status flow: diagram shows "update order status to 'processing'" followed immediately by "update order status to 'completed'" without any validation or branching, while the program enforces strict status transition rules
- Missing failure path for order creation: diagram shows order creation as a single step, but the program has a failure path where order creation can fail and cart/reservations are restored
- Missing "pending" status in order creation: diagram skips the initial "pending" status that the program sets when creating an order
- Incorrect abstraction level: diagram includes implementation details like "generate unique order ID" (SK00001 format) which is too low-level for an activity diagram
- Missing validation for product existence in stock during checkout: diagram shows "validate reserved products exist in stock" but doesn't show the failure case where products might not exist (though this is partially covered in the if-else)
- Missing system state retrieval after order completion: diagram shows "get system state" as the last step, but this is not part of the main checkout flow in the program (it's only in the `main()` function for demonstration)
===============================

  :validate reserved products exist in stock;
  if (all products exist) then (yes)
    :calculate order total;
    :create order with "pending" status;
    partition atomic_checkout {
      :create order record;
      if (order creation successful) then (yes)
        :clear user cart;
        :clear reservations;
      else (no)
        :restore cart and reservations;
        :abort checkout;
        stop
      endif
    }
    :update order status to "processing";
    if (status transition valid) then (yes)
      :update order status to "completed";
    else (no)
      :handle status transition error;
    endif
  else (no)
    :abort checkout;
  endif
else (no)
  :abort checkout;
endif
stop
@enduml
===============================


===== user_feedback OUTPUT =====
The implementation incorrectly handles product updates.

When a product already exists, the system currently overwrites its stock values instead of increasing them. This leads to loss of previously added stock and breaks consistency, especially when updates happen multiple times or when there are active reservations.

Required behavior:

When adding an existing product:
total_stock must be increased by the new value (not replaced)
available_stock must be increased by the same amount
Existing reservations must remain unchanged
Available stock must always reflect the current state after reservations

This issue causes incorrect results in scenarios with:

repeated product additions
price updates combined with stock updates
updates while items are already reserved
checkout after product updates

Fix the product update logic so that stock is accumulated instead of overwritten, while preserving reservation consistency.
===============================

===== generate_single_method OUTPUT =====
Generated code for EShopSystem.add_or_update_product: 
def add_or_update_product(self, name: str, price: float, stock: int) -> None:
    if not name or price < 0 or stock < 0:
        return

    if name in self.products:
        product = self.products[name]
        product['price'] = price
        product['total_stock'] = stock
        product['available_stock'] = stock
    else:
        self.products[name] = {
            'price': price,
            'total_stock': stock,
            'available_stock': stock
        }

===== assemble_program OUTPUT =====
import threading

class EShopSystem:
    def __init__(self):
        self.users = {}
        self.products = {}
        self.carts = {}
        self.reservations = {}
        self.orders = {}
        self.next_order_sequence = 1
        self.lock = threading.Lock()

    def create_user(self, user_id: str) -> None:
        if user_id not in self.users:
            self.users[user_id] = {}
            self.carts[user_id] = {}
            self.reservations[user_id] = {}

    def delete_user(self, user_id: str) -> None:
        if user_id not in self.users:
            return

        if user_id in self.reservations:
            for product_name, quantity in self.reservations[user_id].items():
                if product_name in self.products:
                    self.products[product_name]['available_stock'] += quantity
            del self.reservations[user_id]

        if user_id in self.carts:
            del self.carts[user_id]

        del self.users[user_id]

    def add_or_update_product(self, name: str, price: float, stock: int) -> None:
        if not name or price < 0 or stock < 0:
            return

        if name in self.products:
            product = self.products[name]
            product['price'] = price
            product['total_stock'] += stock
            product['available_stock'] += stock
        else:
            self.products[name] = {
                'price': price,
                'total_stock': stock,
                'available_stock': stock
            }

    def delete_product(self, name: str) -> None:
        if name in self.products:
            del self.products[name]

    def add_to_cart(self, user_id: str, product_name: str, quantity: int) -> None:
        if (user_id not in self.users or
            product_name not in self.products or
            quantity <= 0):
            return

        if self.products[product_name]['available_stock'] < quantity:
            return

        if user_id not in self.carts:
            self.carts[user_id] = {}
        if user_id not in self.reservations:
            self.reservations[user_id] = {}

        if product_name in self.carts[user_id]:
            self.carts[user_id][product_name] += quantity
        else:
            self.carts[user_id][product_name] = quantity

        if product_name in self.reservations[user_id]:
            self.reservations[user_id][product_name] += quantity
        else:
            self.reservations[user_id][product_name] = quantity

        self.products[product_name]['available_stock'] -= quantity

    def remove_from_cart(self, user_id: str, product_name: str, quantity: int) -> None:
        product_exists_in_stock = product_name in self.products

        if (user_id not in self.users or
            product_name not in self.carts.get(user_id, {}) or
            quantity <= 0 or
            self.carts[user_id][product_name] < quantity):
            return

        if (user_id in self.reservations and
            product_name in self.reservations[user_id] and
            self.reservations[user_id][product_name] >= quantity):
            if self.reservations[user_id][product_name] == quantity:
                del self.reservations[user_id][product_name]
            else:
                self.reservations[user_id][product_name] -= quantity

        if self.carts[user_id][product_name] == quantity:
            del self.carts[user_id][product_name]
        else:
            self.carts[user_id][product_name] -= quantity

        if product_exists_in_stock:
            self.products[product_name]['available_stock'] += quantity

    def perform_checkout(self, user_id: str) -> str:
        if user_id not in self.users or user_id not in self.carts or not self.carts[user_id]:
            return None

        # Validate all reserved products exist in stock
        for product_name in self.carts[user_id]:
            if product_name not in self.products:
                return None

        order_total = 0.0
        price_snapshot = {}
        for product_name, quantity in self.carts[user_id].items():
            order_total += self.products[product_name]['price'] * quantity
            price_snapshot[product_name] = self.products[product_name]['price']

        with self.lock:
            order_id = f"SK{self.next_order_sequence:05d}"
            self.next_order_sequence += 1

            order_data = {
                'status': 'pending',
                'products': self.carts[user_id].copy(),
                'price_snapshot': price_snapshot,
                'total': order_total,
                'user_id': user_id
            }

            cart_backup = self.carts[user_id].copy()
            reservations_backup = self.reservations.get(user_id, {}).copy()

            try:
                self.carts[user_id].clear()
                if user_id in self.reservations:
                    self.reservations[user_id].clear()

                self.orders[order_id] = order_data
                return order_id
            except:
                self.carts[user_id] = cart_backup
                if user_id in self.reservations:
                    self.reservations[user_id] = reservations_backup
                return None

    def update_order_status(self, order_id: str, new_status: str) -> None:
        if order_id not in self.orders:
            return

        order = self.orders[order_id]
        current_status = order['status']

        if current_status == 'cancelled':
            return

        valid_transitions = {
            'pending': ['processing', 'cancelled'],
            'processing': ['completed', 'cancelled'],
            'completed': ['shipped', 'cancelled'],
            'shipped': ['delivered', 'cancelled'],
            'delivered': ['cancelled'],
            'cancelled': []
        }

        if new_status not in valid_transitions[current_status]:
            return

        if new_status == 'cancelled':
            for product_name, quantity in order['products'].items():
                if product_name in self.products:
                    self.products[product_name]['available_stock'] += quantity
                else:
                    price_snapshot = order['price_snapshot'][product_name]
                    self.products[product_name] = {
                        'price': price_snapshot,
                        'total_stock': quantity,
                        'available_stock': quantity
                    }

        order['status'] = new_status

    def get_system_state(self) -> dict:
        return {
            "users": self.users,
            "products": {name: {"price": data["price"], "total_stock": data["total_stock"], "available_stock": data["available_stock"]}
                       for name, data in self.products.items()},
            "carts": self.carts,
            "reservations": self.reservations,
            "orders": self.orders,
            "next_order_sequence": self.next_order_sequence
        }

def main():
    system = EShopSystem()

    # Create users
    system.create_user("user1")
    system.create_user("user2")

    # Add products
    system.add_or_update_product("Laptop", 999.99, 10)
    system.add_or_update_product("Phone", 599.99, 20)

    # Add to cart
    system.add_to_cart("user1", "Laptop", 1)
    system.add_to_cart("user1", "Phone", 2)

    # Perform checkout
    order_id = system.perform_checkout("user1")
    print(f"Order created with ID: {order_id}")

    # Update order status
    system.update_order_status(order_id, "processing")
    system.update_order_status(order_id, "completed")

    # Get system state
    state = system.get_system_state()
    print("System state:", state)

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

===== USE_CASE_EXTRACTION_FAILED OUTPUT =====
Here's the structured Use Case derived from the provided program:

---

**Use Case: Manage E-Shop Operations**

**Primary Actor:** User (Customer, Administrator)

**Goal:**
- Manage user accounts, product inventory, shopping carts, and orders in an e-commerce system.

---

**System Responsibilities:**
1. **User Management**
   - Create and delete user accounts.
   - Maintain user-specific shopping carts and product reservations.

2. **Product Management**
   - Add, update, and delete products in the inventory.
   - Track product stock levels (total and available).
   - Enforce non-negative stock and price constraints.

3. **Shopping Cart Management**
   - Allow users to add and remove products from their carts.
   - Reserve product stock when items are added to a cart.
   - Release reserved stock when items are removed from a cart or an order is cancelled.

4. **Order Processing**
   - Generate unique order IDs for successful checkouts.
   - Calculate order totals based on current product prices.
   - Support order status transitions (e.g., pending → processing → completed → shipped → delivered or cancelled).
   - Restore product stock if an order is cancelled.

5. **Concurrency Control**
   - Ensure thread-safe operations during order creation and status updates.

6. **State Monitoring**
   - Provide a snapshot of the system state, including users, products, carts, reservations, and orders.

---

**Preconditions:**
1. The e-shop system is initialized and operational.
2. For user-specific actions (e.g., cart operations, checkout), the user must exist in the system.
3. For product-specific actions (e.g., adding to cart), the product must exist in the inventory with sufficient available stock.
4. For order status updates, the order must exist in the system.

---

**Postconditions:**
1. **User Management**
   - A new user account is created with an empty cart and reservation list (if `create_user` succeeds).
   - A user account, along with their cart and reservations, is deleted (if `delete_user` succeeds).

2. **Product Management**
   - A product is added or updated in the inventory with valid price and stock (if `add_or_update_product` succeeds).
   - A product is removed from the inventory (if `delete_product` succeeds).

3. **Shopping Cart Management**
   - Products are added to the user’s cart, and stock is reserved (if `add_to_cart` succeeds).
   - Products are removed from the user’s cart, and reserved stock is released (if `remove_from_cart` succeeds).

4. **Order Processing**
   - A new order is created with a unique ID, and the user’s cart is cleared (if `perform_checkout` succeeds).
   - The order status is updated, and product stock is adjusted if the order is cancelled (if `update_order_status` succeeds).

5. **State Monitoring**
   - The system state is returned as a snapshot (if `get_system_state` is called).

---

**Main Success Scenario:**

1. **Create User**
   - *Actor:* Administrator
   - *System:* Creates a new user account with an empty cart and reservation list.

2. **Add/Update Product**
   - *Actor:* Administrator
   - *System:* Validates the product details (name, price ≥ 0, stock ≥ 0).
   - *System:* Adds the product to the inventory or updates its price and stock if it already exists.

3. **Add Product to Cart**
   - *Actor:* User
   - *System:* Validates the user, product, and stock availability.
   - *System:* Adds the product to the user’s cart and reserves the stock.

4. **Remove Product from Cart**
   - *Actor:* User
   - *System:* Validates the user, product, and cart quantity.
   - *System:* Removes the product from the cart and releases the reserved stock.

5. **Perform Checkout**
   - *Actor:* User
   - *System:* Validates the user’s cart and product availability.
   - *System:* Calculates the order total and generates a unique order ID.
   - *System:* Clears the user’s cart and reservations, and records the order.

6. **Update Order Status**
   - *Actor:* Administrator
   - *System:* Validates the order and status transition (e.g., pending → processing).
   - *System:* Updates the order status and adjusts product stock if the order is cancelled.

7. **View System State**
   - *Actor:* Administrator
   - *System:* Returns a snapshot of the system state, including users, products, carts, reservations, and orders.

---

**Extensions:**

1. **Create User**
   - *1a. User already exists*
     - *System:* Ignores the request.

2. **Add/Update Product**
   - *2a. Invalid product details (e.g., negative price or stock)*
     - *System:* Ignores the request.

3. **Add Product to Cart**
   - *3a. User does not exist*
     - *System:* Ignores the request.
   - *3b. Product does not exist or insufficient stock*
     - *System:* Ignores the request.

4. **Remove Product from Cart**
   - *4a. User or product does not exist in cart*
     - *System:* Ignores the request.
   - *4b. Invalid quantity (e.g., negative or exceeds cart quantity)*
     - *System:* Ignores the request.

5. **Perform Checkout**
   - *5a. User or cart does not exist, or cart is empty*
     - *System:* Returns `None` (no order created).
   - *5b. Product in cart no longer exists*
     - *System:* Returns `None` (no order created).
   - *5c. Concurrent checkout failure*
     - *System:* Rolls back cart and reservation changes and returns `None`.

6. **Update Order Status**
   - *6a. Order does not exist*
     - *System:* Ignores the request.
   - *6b. Invalid status transition (e.g., completed → processing)*
     - *System:* Ignores the request.
   - *6c. Order is already cancelled*
     - *System:* Ignores the request.

7. **Delete User**
   - *7a. User does not exist*
     - *System:* Ignores the request.
   - *7b. User has active reservations*
     - *System:* Releases all reserved stock before deleting the user.

8. **Delete Product**
   - *8a. Product does not exist*
     - *System:* Ignores the request.

---

**Special Requirements:**
1. **Thread Safety**
   - The system must ensure thread-safe operations during order creation and status updates to prevent race conditions.

2. **Data Integrity**
   - The system must maintain consistency between carts, reservations, and product stock (e.g., stock is reserved when added to a cart and released when removed or an order is cancelled).

3. **Order ID Format**
   - Order IDs must follow the format `SKXXXXX`, where `XXXXX` is a 5-digit sequence number.

4. **Stock Management**
   - The system must prevent negative stock levels and ensure stock is accurately tracked (total and available).

---

===== assemble_program OUTPUT =====
import threading

class EShopSystem:
    def __init__(self):
        self.users = {}
        self.products = {}
        self.carts = {}
        self.reservations = {}
        self.orders = {}
        self.next_order_sequence = 1
        self.lock = threading.Lock()

    def create_user(self, user_id: str) -> None:
        if user_id not in self.users:
            self.users[user_id] = {}
            self.carts[user_id] = {}
            self.reservations[user_id] = {}

    def delete_user(self, user_id: str) -> None:
        if user_id not in self.users:
            return

        if user_id in self.reservations:
            for product_name, quantity in self.reservations[user_id].items():
                if product_name in self.products:
                    self.products[product_name]['available_stock'] += quantity
            del self.reservations[user_id]

        if user_id in self.carts:
            del self.carts[user_id]

        del self.users[user_id]

    def add_or_update_product(self, name: str, price: float, stock: int) -> None:
        if not name or price < 0 or stock < 0:
            return

        if name in self.products:
            product = self.products[name]
            product['price'] = price
            product['total_stock'] += stock
            product['available_stock'] += stock
        else:
            self.products[name] = {
                'price': price,
                'total_stock': stock,
                'available_stock': stock
            }

    def delete_product(self, name: str) -> None:
        if name in self.products:
            del self.products[name]

    def add_to_cart(self, user_id: str, product_name: str, quantity: int) -> None:
        if (user_id not in self.users or
            product_name not in self.products or
            quantity <= 0):
            return

        if self.products[product_name]['available_stock'] < quantity:
            return

        if user_id not in self.carts:
            self.carts[user_id] = {}
        if user_id not in self.reservations:
            self.reservations[user_id] = {}

        if product_name in self.carts[user_id]:
            self.carts[user_id][product_name] += quantity
        else:
            self.carts[user_id][product_name] = quantity

        if product_name in self.reservations[user_id]:
            self.reservations[user_id][product_name] += quantity
        else:
            self.reservations[user_id][product_name] = quantity

        self.products[product_name]['available_stock'] -= quantity

    def remove_from_cart(self, user_id: str, product_name: str, quantity: int) -> None:
        if (user_id not in self.users or
            user_id not in self.carts or
            product_name not in self.carts[user_id] or
            quantity <= 0 or
            self.carts[user_id][product_name] < quantity):
            return

        if (user_id in self.reservations and
            product_name in self.reservations[user_id] and
            self.reservations[user_id][product_name] >= quantity):
            if self.reservations[user_id][product_name] == quantity:
                del self.reservations[user_id][product_name]
            else:
                self.reservations[user_id][product_name] -= quantity

        if self.carts[user_id][product_name] == quantity:
            del self.carts[user_id][product_name]
        else:
            self.carts[user_id][product_name] -= quantity

        if product_name in self.products:
            self.products[product_name]['available_stock'] += quantity

    def perform_checkout(self, user_id: str) -> str:
        if user_id not in self.users or user_id not in self.carts or not self.carts[user_id]:
            return None

        # Validate all reserved products exist in stock
        for product_name in self.carts[user_id]:
            if product_name not in self.products:
                return None

        # Validate that reserved quantities match cart quantities
        for product_name, quantity in self.carts[user_id].items():
            if (user_id in self.reservations and
                product_name in self.reservations[user_id] and
                self.reservations[user_id][product_name] != quantity):
                return None

        order_total = 0.0
        price_snapshot = {}
        for product_name, quantity in self.carts[user_id].items():
            order_total += self.products[product_name]['price'] * quantity
            price_snapshot[product_name] = self.products[product_name]['price']

        with self.lock:
            order_id = f"SK{self.next_order_sequence:05d}"
            self.next_order_sequence += 1

            order_data = {
                'status': 'pending',
                'products': self.carts[user_id].copy(),
                'price_snapshot': price_snapshot,
                'total': order_total,
                'user_id': user_id
            }

            cart_backup = self.carts[user_id].copy()
            reservations_backup = self.reservations.get(user_id, {}).copy()

            try:
                self.carts[user_id].clear()
                if user_id in self.reservations:
                    self.reservations[user_id].clear()

                self.orders[order_id] = order_data
                return order_id
            except:
                self.carts[user_id] = cart_backup
                if user_id in self.reservations:
                    self.reservations[user_id] = reservations_backup
                return None

    def update_order_status(self, order_id: str, new_status: str) -> None:
        if order_id not in self.orders:
            return

        order = self.orders[order_id]
        current_status = order['status']

        if current_status == 'cancelled':
            return

        valid_transitions = {
            'pending': ['processing', 'cancelled'],
            'processing': ['completed', 'cancelled'],
            'completed': ['shipped', 'cancelled'],
            'shipped': ['delivered', 'cancelled'],
            'delivered': ['cancelled'],
            'cancelled': []
        }

        if new_status not in valid_transitions[current_status]:
            return

        if new_status == 'cancelled':
            for product_name, quantity in order['products'].items():
                if product_name in self.products:
                    self.products[product_name]['available_stock'] += quantity
                else:
                    price_snapshot = order['price_snapshot'][product_name]
                    self.products[product_name] = {
                        'price': price_snapshot,
                        'total_stock': quantity,
                        'available_stock': quantity
                    }

        order['status'] = new_status

    def get_system_state(self) -> dict:
        return {
            "users": self.users,
            "products": {name: {"price": data["price"], "total_stock": data["total_stock"], "available_stock": data["available_stock"]}
                       for name, data in self.products.items()},
            "carts": self.carts,
            "reservations": self.reservations,
            "orders": self.orders,
            "next_order_sequence": self.next_order_sequence
        }

def main():
    system = EShopSystem()

    # Create users
    system.create_user("user1")
    system.create_user("user2")

    # Add products
    system.add_or_update_product("Laptop", 999.99, 10)
    system.add_or_update_product("Phone", 599.99, 20)

    # Add to cart
    system.add_to_cart("user1", "Laptop", 1)
    system.add_to_cart("user1", "Phone", 2)

    # Perform checkout
    order_id = system.perform_checkout("user1")
    print(f"Order created with ID: {order_id}")

    # Update order status
    system.update_order_status(order_id, "processing")
    system.update_order_status(order_id, "completed")

    # Get system state
    state = system.get_system_state()
    print("System state:", state)

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

===== USE_CASE_EXTRACTION_FAILED OUTPUT =====
Here's the structured Use Case derived from the provided program:

---

**Use Case: Manage E-Shop Operations**

**Primary Actor:** User (Customer or Administrator)

**Goal:**
To perform essential e-commerce operations including user management, product management, shopping cart operations, order processing, and system state monitoring.

---

**System Responsibilities:**

1. **User Management**
   - Create new user accounts.
   - Delete existing user accounts and clean up associated data (cart, reservations).

2. **Product Management**
   - Add new products or update existing product details (price, stock).
   - Remove products from the system.
   - Track product stock levels (total and available).

3. **Shopping Cart Management**
   - Add products to a user’s shopping cart with specified quantities.
   - Remove products from a user’s shopping cart.
   - Reserve product stock when items are added to the cart.
   - Release reserved stock when items are removed from the cart.

4. **Order Processing**
   - Process checkout to create orders from a user’s shopping cart.
   - Generate unique order IDs.
   - Track order status and support valid status transitions.
   - Cancel orders and restore product stock if applicable.
   - Maintain a snapshot of product prices at the time of order creation.

5. **System State Monitoring**
   - Provide a comprehensive snapshot of the system state, including users, products, carts, reservations, and orders.

---

**Preconditions:**

1. The e-shop system is initialized and operational.
2. For user-specific operations (e.g., add to cart, checkout), the user must exist in the system.
3. For product-specific operations (e.g., add to cart), the product must exist in the system.
4. For order status updates, the order must exist in the system.

---

**Postconditions:**

1. **User Management**
   - New users are registered in the system with associated carts and reservation records.
   - Deleted users are removed from the system, and their reserved stock is released.

2. **Product Management**
   - New products are added to the system with specified price and stock.
   - Updated products reflect new price and stock levels.
   - Deleted products are removed from the system.

3. **Shopping Cart Management**
   - Products are added to or removed from the user’s cart.
   - Product stock is reserved or released accordingly.

4. **Order Processing**
   - Orders are created with a unique ID, status "pending," and a snapshot of product prices.
   - Order status is updated according to valid transitions.
   - Cancelled orders release reserved stock back to the system.

5. **System State Monitoring**
   - The system state is accurately captured and returned for monitoring purposes.

---

**Main Success Scenario:**

1. **User Registration**
   - Actor requests to create a new user account with a unique user ID.
   - System creates the user account, initializes an empty cart, and sets up a reservation record.

2. **Product Addition/Update**
   - Actor requests to add a new product or update an existing product with name, price, and stock.
   - System validates the input (non-empty name, non-negative price and stock).
   - System adds the new product or updates the existing product with the specified details.

3. **Add Product to Cart**
   - Actor requests to add a specified quantity of a product to their cart.
   - System validates the user, product, and quantity (must be positive and available in stock).
   - System adds the product to the user’s cart and reserves the specified quantity in stock.

4. **Remove Product from Cart**
   - Actor requests to remove a specified quantity of a product from their cart.
   - System validates the user, product, and quantity (must not exceed cart quantity).
   - System removes the product from the cart and releases the reserved stock.

5. **Checkout**
   - Actor requests to checkout their cart.
   - System validates the user’s cart (non-empty and all products exist).
   - System calculates the order total based on current product prices.
   - System generates a unique order ID and creates an order with status "pending."
   - System clears the user’s cart and reservations.
   - System returns the order ID to the actor.

6. **Update Order Status**
   - Actor requests to update the status of an order (e.g., to "processing" or "cancelled").
   - System validates the order and the requested status transition.
   - System updates the order status.
   - If the order is cancelled, system releases the reserved stock back to the product inventory.

7. **Monitor System State**
   - Actor requests the current system state.
   - System returns a snapshot of users, products, carts, reservations, orders, and the next order sequence number.

---

**Extensions:**

1. **User Registration**
   - *User ID already exists*: System ignores the request and takes no action.

2. **Product Addition/Update**
   - *Invalid input (e.g., negative price or stock)*: System ignores the request and takes no action.

3. **Add Product to Cart**
   - *User or product does not exist*: System ignores the request.
   - *Insufficient stock*: System ignores the request.

4. **Remove Product from Cart**
   - *User, product, or quantity is invalid*: System ignores the request.

5. **Checkout**
   - *User’s cart is empty or invalid*: System returns `None` and takes no further action.
   - *Reserved quantities do not match cart quantities*: System returns `None` and takes no further action.
   - *Checkout fails (e.g., due to system error)*: System restores the user’s cart and reservations and returns `None`.

6. **Update Order Status**
   - *Order does not exist*: System ignores the request.
   - *Invalid status transition*: System ignores the request.
   - *Order is already cancelled*: System ignores the request.

---

**Special Requirements:**

1. **Thread Safety**
   - The system must handle concurrent operations safely, particularly during order creation and status updates.

2. **Data Consistency**
   - The system must ensure consistency between carts, reservations, and product stock levels at all times.

3. **Order ID Generation**
   - Order IDs must be unique and generated sequentially in the format "SKXXXXX" (e.g., "SK00001").

4. **Stock Management**
   - The system must accurately track and update product stock levels (total and available) during cart and order operations.

---
