===== assemble_program OUTPUT =====
import threading

class EShopSystem:
    def __init__(self):
        self.users = {}
        self.products = {}
        self.carts = {}
        self.reservations = {}
        self.orders = {}
        self.next_order_sequence = 1
        self.lock = threading.Lock()

    def create_user(self, user_id: str) -> None:
        if user_id not in self.users:
            self.users[user_id] = {}
            self.carts[user_id] = {}
            self.reservations[user_id] = {}

    def delete_user(self, user_id: str) -> None:
        if user_id not in self.users:
            return

        if user_id in self.reservations:
            for product_name, quantity in self.reservations[user_id].items():
                if product_name in self.products:
                    self.products[product_name]['available_stock'] += quantity
            del self.reservations[user_id]

        if user_id in self.carts:
            del self.carts[user_id]

        del self.users[user_id]

    def add_or_update_product(self, name: str, price: float, stock: int) -> None:
        if not name or price < 0 or stock < 0:
            return

        if name in self.products:
            product = self.products[name]
            product['price'] = price
            product['total_stock'] += stock
            # Only increase available_stock if there are no active reservations
            if not any(user_id in self.reservations and name in self.reservations[user_id]
                      for user_id in self.users):
                product['available_stock'] += stock
        else:
            self.products[name] = {
                'price': price,
                'total_stock': stock,
                'available_stock': stock
            }

    def delete_product(self, name: str) -> None:
        if name in self.products:
            del self.products[name]

    def add_to_cart(self, user_id: str, product_name: str, quantity: int) -> None:
        if (user_id not in self.users or
            product_name not in self.products or
            quantity <= 0):
            return

        if self.products[product_name]['available_stock'] < quantity:
            return

        if user_id not in self.carts:
            self.carts[user_id] = {}
        if user_id not in self.reservations:
            self.reservations[user_id] = {}

        if product_name in self.carts[user_id]:
            self.carts[user_id][product_name] += quantity
        else:
            self.carts[user_id][product_name] = quantity

        if product_name in self.reservations[user_id]:
            self.reservations[user_id][product_name] += quantity
        else:
            self.reservations[user_id][product_name] = quantity

        self.products[product_name]['available_stock'] -= quantity

    def remove_from_cart(self, user_id: str, product_name: str, quantity: int) -> None:
        if (user_id not in self.users or
            user_id not in self.carts or
            product_name not in self.carts[user_id] or
            quantity <= 0 or
            self.carts[user_id][product_name] < quantity):
            return

        if (user_id in self.reservations and
            product_name in self.reservations[user_id] and
            self.reservations[user_id][product_name] >= quantity):
            if self.reservations[user_id][product_name] == quantity:
                del self.reservations[user_id][product_name]
            else:
                self.reservations[user_id][product_name] -= quantity

        if self.carts[user_id][product_name] == quantity:
            del self.carts[user_id][product_name]
        else:
            self.carts[user_id][product_name] -= quantity

        if product_name in self.products:
            self.products[product_name]['available_stock'] += quantity

    def perform_checkout(self, user_id: str) -> str:
        if user_id not in self.users or user_id not in self.carts or not self.carts[user_id]:
            return None

        # Validate all reserved products exist in stock
        for product_name in self.carts[user_id]:
            if product_name not in self.products:
                return None

        # Validate that reserved quantities match cart quantities
        for product_name, quantity in self.carts[user_id].items():
            if (user_id in self.reservations and
                product_name in self.reservations[user_id] and
                self.reservations[user_id][product_name] != quantity):
                return None

        order_total = 0.0
        price_snapshot = {}
        for product_name, quantity in self.carts[user_id].items():
            order_total += self.products[product_name]['price'] * quantity
            price_snapshot[product_name] = self.products[product_name]['price']

        with self.lock:
            order_id = f"SK{self.next_order_sequence:05d}"
            self.next_order_sequence += 1

            order_data = {
                'status': 'pending',
                'products': self.carts[user_id].copy(),
                'price_snapshot': price_snapshot,
                'total': order_total,
                'user_id': user_id
            }

            cart_backup = self.carts[user_id].copy()
            reservations_backup = self.reservations.get(user_id, {}).copy()

            try:
                self.carts[user_id].clear()
                if user_id in self.reservations:
                    self.reservations[user_id].clear()

                self.orders[order_id] = order_data
                return order_id
            except:
                self.carts[user_id] = cart_backup
                if user_id in self.reservations:
                    self.reservations[user_id] = reservations_backup
                return None

    def update_order_status(self, order_id: str, new_status: str) -> None:
        if order_id not in self.orders:
            return

        order = self.orders[order_id]
        current_status = order['status']

        if current_status == 'cancelled':
            return

        valid_transitions = {
            'pending': ['processing', 'cancelled'],
            'processing': ['completed', 'cancelled'],
            'completed': ['shipped', 'cancelled'],
            'shipped': ['delivered', 'cancelled'],
            'delivered': ['cancelled'],
            'cancelled': []
        }

        if new_status not in valid_transitions[current_status]:
            return

        if new_status == 'cancelled':
            for product_name, quantity in order['products'].items():
                if product_name in self.products:
                    self.products[product_name]['available_stock'] += quantity
                else:
                    price_snapshot = order['price_snapshot'][product_name]
                    self.products[product_name] = {
                        'price': price_snapshot,
                        'total_stock': quantity,
                        'available_stock': quantity
                    }

        order['status'] = new_status

    def get_system_state(self) -> dict:
        return {
            "users": self.users,
            "products": {name: {"price": data["price"], "total_stock": data["total_stock"], "available_stock": data["available_stock"]}
                       for name, data in self.products.items()},
            "carts": self.carts,
            "reservations": self.reservations,
            "orders": self.orders,
            "next_order_sequence": self.next_order_sequence
        }

def main():
    system = EShopSystem()

    # Create users
    system.create_user("user1")
    system.create_user("user2")

    # Add products
    system.add_or_update_product("Laptop", 999.99, 10)
    system.add_or_update_product("Phone", 599.99, 20)

    # Add to cart
    system.add_to_cart("user1", "Laptop", 1)
    system.add_to_cart("user1", "Phone", 2)

    # Perform checkout
    order_id = system.perform_checkout("user1")
    print(f"Order created with ID: {order_id}")

    # Update order status
    system.update_order_status(order_id, "processing")
    system.update_order_status(order_id, "completed")

    # Get system state
    state = system.get_system_state()
    print("System state:", state)

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

===== USE_CASE_EXTRACTION_FAILED OUTPUT =====
Here's the structured Use Case derived from the provided program:

---

**Use Case: Manage E-Shop Operations**

**Primary Actor:** Customer / Administrator

**Goal:**
- Customer: Purchase products through the e-shop system
- Administrator: Manage users, products, and orders in the e-shop system

---

**System Responsibilities:**
1. **User Management**
   - Create and delete user accounts
   - Maintain user-specific shopping carts and product reservations

2. **Product Management**
   - Add, update, and delete products with pricing and stock information
   - Track total and available stock for each product
   - Adjust available stock when products are reserved or released

3. **Shopping Cart Management**
   - Add and remove products from user carts
   - Reserve products in stock when added to cart
   - Release reservations when products are removed from cart

4. **Order Processing**
   - Process checkout to create orders from user carts
   - Generate unique order IDs
   - Maintain order status with valid transitions
   - Handle order cancellation and stock adjustments

5. **Concurrency Control**
   - Ensure thread-safe operations for order creation and stock management

6. **System State Monitoring**
   - Provide a snapshot of current system state including users, products, carts, reservations, and orders

---

**Preconditions:**
1. System is initialized with empty user, product, cart, reservation, and order stores
2. For user-specific operations, the user must exist in the system
3. For product-specific operations, the product must exist in the system (unless adding a new product)
4. For cart operations, the user must have an active cart
5. For checkout, the user must have items in their cart with valid reservations

---

**Postconditions:**
1. **User Management**
   - New user is created with an empty cart and reservation store
   - Deleted user is removed along with their cart, reservations, and any pending orders are canceled

2. **Product Management**
   - New product is added with specified price and stock
   - Updated product reflects new price and adjusted stock
   - Deleted product is removed from the system

3. **Shopping Cart Management**
   - Products are added/removed from user cart with corresponding stock reservations
   - Available stock is adjusted based on cart operations

4. **Order Processing**
   - Successful checkout creates an order with "pending" status
   - Order status transitions follow valid state machine
   - Canceled orders release reserved stock back to available inventory

5. **System State**
   - All operations maintain consistent system state
   - Thread-safe operations prevent race conditions

---

**Main Success Scenario:**

1. **User Registration**
   - Actor requests to create a new user account
   - System creates user account with empty cart and reservation store

2. **Product Addition**
   - Administrator adds a new product with name, price, and stock
   - System stores product with specified details and available stock

3. **Product Browsing & Cart Addition**
   - Customer requests to add product to cart with specified quantity
   - System validates product availability
   - System reserves product quantity and adds to customer's cart

4. **Checkout Process**
   - Customer requests to checkout their cart
   - System validates all cart items are available and reservations match
   - System calculates order total based on current prices
   - System generates unique order ID
   - System creates order with "pending" status
   - System clears customer's cart and reservations
   - System returns order ID to customer

5. **Order Status Update**
   - Administrator updates order status (e.g., to "processing")
   - System validates status transition
   - System updates order status

6. **Order Completion**
   - Administrator updates order status to "completed"
   - System maintains order in completed state

---

**Extensions:**

1a. **User Already Exists**
   - If user ID already exists during creation, system ignores request

2a. **Invalid Product Details**
   - If product name is empty, price is negative, or stock is negative during addition/update, system ignores request

3a. **Insufficient Stock**
   - If requested quantity exceeds available stock during cart addition, system ignores request

3b. **Product Not Found**
   - If product doesn't exist during cart addition, system ignores request

4a. **Empty Cart**
   - If customer attempts checkout with empty cart, system returns no order ID

4b. **Product Unavailable**
   - If any cart item is no longer available during checkout, system returns no order ID

4c. **Reservation Mismatch**
   - If cart quantities don't match reservations during checkout, system returns no order ID

5a. **Invalid Status Transition**
   - If requested status transition is invalid, system ignores request

5b. **Order Cancellation**
   - If order is canceled, system releases reserved stock back to available inventory

6a. **User Deletion**
   - If user is deleted, system cancels any pending reservations and removes user data

---

**Special Requirements:**
1. The system must handle concurrent operations safely, particularly during order creation and stock management
2. Order IDs must be generated sequentially with a consistent format (SK followed by 5-digit number)
3. All operations must maintain system state consistency
4. Stock adjustments must be atomic to prevent race conditions

---

===== assemble_program OUTPUT =====
import threading

class EShopSystem:
    def __init__(self):
        self.users = {}
        self.products = {}
        self.carts = {}
        self.reservations = {}
        self.orders = {}
        self.next_order_sequence = 1
        self.lock = threading.Lock()

    def create_user(self, user_id: str) -> None:
        if user_id not in self.users:
            self.users[user_id] = {}
            self.carts[user_id] = {}
            self.reservations[user_id] = {}

    def delete_user(self, user_id: str) -> None:
        if user_id not in self.users:
            return

        if user_id in self.reservations:
            for product_name, quantity in self.reservations[user_id].items():
                if product_name in self.products:
                    self.products[product_name]['available_stock'] += quantity
            del self.reservations[user_id]

        if user_id in self.carts:
            del self.carts[user_id]

        del self.users[user_id]

    def add_or_update_product(self, name: str, price: float, stock: int) -> None:
        if not name or price < 0 or stock < 0:
            return

        if name in self.products:
            product = self.products[name]
            product['price'] = price
            product['total_stock'] += stock
            product['available_stock'] += stock
        else:
            self.products[name] = {
                'price': price,
                'total_stock': stock,
                'available_stock': stock
            }

    def delete_product(self, name: str) -> None:
        if name in self.products:
            del self.products[name]

    def add_to_cart(self, user_id: str, product_name: str, quantity: int) -> None:
        if (user_id not in self.users or
            product_name not in self.products or
            quantity <= 0):
            return

        if self.products[product_name]['available_stock'] < quantity:
            return

        if user_id not in self.carts:
            self.carts[user_id] = {}
        if user_id not in self.reservations:
            self.reservations[user_id] = {}

        if product_name in self.carts[user_id]:
            self.carts[user_id][product_name] += quantity
        else:
            self.carts[user_id][product_name] = quantity

        if product_name in self.reservations[user_id]:
            self.reservations[user_id][product_name] += quantity
        else:
            self.reservations[user_id][product_name] = quantity

        self.products[product_name]['available_stock'] -= quantity

    def remove_from_cart(self, user_id: str, product_name: str, quantity: int) -> None:
        if (user_id not in self.users or
            user_id not in self.carts or
            product_name not in self.carts[user_id] or
            quantity <= 0 or
            self.carts[user_id][product_name] < quantity):
            return

        if (user_id in self.reservations and
            product_name in self.reservations[user_id] and
            self.reservations[user_id][product_name] >= quantity):
            if self.reservations[user_id][product_name] == quantity:
                del self.reservations[user_id][product_name]
            else:
                self.reservations[user_id][product_name] -= quantity

        if self.carts[user_id][product_name] == quantity:
            del self.carts[user_id][product_name]
        else:
            self.carts[user_id][product_name] -= quantity

        if product_name in self.products:
            self.products[product_name]['available_stock'] += quantity

    def perform_checkout(self, user_id: str) -> str:
        if user_id not in self.users or user_id not in self.carts or not self.carts[user_id]:
            return None

        # Validate all reserved products exist in stock
        for product_name in self.carts[user_id]:
            if product_name not in self.products:
                return None

        # Validate that reserved quantities match cart quantities
        for product_name, quantity in self.carts[user_id].items():
            if (user_id in self.reservations and
                product_name in self.reservations[user_id] and
                self.reservations[user_id][product_name] != quantity):
                return None

        order_total = 0.0
        price_snapshot = {}
        for product_name, quantity in self.carts[user_id].items():
            order_total += self.products[product_name]['price'] * quantity
            price_snapshot[product_name] = self.products[product_name]['price']

        with self.lock:
            order_id = f"SK{self.next_order_sequence:05d}"
            self.next_order_sequence += 1

            order_data = {
                'status': 'pending',
                'products': self.carts[user_id].copy(),
                'price_snapshot': price_snapshot,
                'total': order_total,
                'user_id': user_id
            }

            cart_backup = self.carts[user_id].copy()
            reservations_backup = self.reservations.get(user_id, {}).copy()

            try:
                self.carts[user_id].clear()
                if user_id in self.reservations:
                    self.reservations[user_id].clear()

                self.orders[order_id] = order_data
                return order_id
            except:
                self.carts[user_id] = cart_backup
                if user_id in self.reservations:
                    self.reservations[user_id] = reservations_backup
                return None

    def update_order_status(self, order_id: str, new_status: str) -> None:
        if order_id not in self.orders:
            return

        order = self.orders[order_id]
        current_status = order['status']

        if current_status == 'cancelled':
            return

        valid_transitions = {
            'pending': ['processing', 'cancelled'],
            'processing': ['completed', 'cancelled'],
            'completed': ['shipped', 'cancelled'],
            'shipped': ['delivered', 'cancelled'],
            'delivered': ['cancelled'],
            'cancelled': []
        }

        if new_status not in valid_transitions[current_status]:
            return

        if new_status == 'cancelled':
            for product_name, quantity in order['products'].items():
                if product_name in self.products:
                    self.products[product_name]['available_stock'] += quantity
                else:
                    price_snapshot = order['price_snapshot'][product_name]
                    self.products[product_name] = {
                        'price': price_snapshot,
                        'total_stock': quantity,
                        'available_stock': quantity
                    }

        order['status'] = new_status

    def get_system_state(self) -> dict:
        return {
            "users": self.users,
            "products": {name: {"price": data["price"], "total_stock": data["total_stock"], "available_stock": data["available_stock"]}
                       for name, data in self.products.items()},
            "carts": self.carts,
            "reservations": self.reservations,
            "orders": self.orders,
            "next_order_sequence": self.next_order_sequence
        }

def main():
    system = EShopSystem()

    # Create users
    system.create_user("user1")
    system.create_user("user2")

    # Add products
    system.add_or_update_product("Laptop", 999.99, 10)
    system.add_or_update_product("Phone", 599.99, 20)

    # Add to cart
    system.add_to_cart("user1", "Laptop", 1)
    system.add_to_cart("user1", "Phone", 2)

    # Perform checkout
    order_id = system.perform_checkout("user1")
    print(f"Order created with ID: {order_id}")

    # Update order status
    system.update_order_status(order_id, "processing")
    system.update_order_status(order_id, "completed")

    # Get system state
    state = system.get_system_state()
    print("System state:", state)

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

===== generate_integration_test OUTPUT =====
def test_main_success_scenario():
    system = EShopSystem()

    # Create user and add product
    system.create_user("user1")
    system.add_or_update_product("laptop", 1000.0, 10)

    # Add product to cart and perform checkout
    system.add_to_cart("user1", "laptop", 2)
    order_id = system.perform_checkout("user1")

    # Verify order creation
    state = system.get_system_state()
    assert order_id in state["orders"]
    assert state["orders"][order_id]["status"] == "pending"
    assert state["users"]["user1"]["cart"] == {}
    assert state["products"]["laptop"]["available_stock"] == 8

def test_alternative_flow_cancel_order():
    system = EShopSystem()

    # Create user and add product
    system.create_user("user2")
    system.add_or_update_product("phone", 500.0, 5)

    # Add product to cart and perform checkout
    system.add_to_cart("user2", "phone", 3)
    order_id = system.perform_checkout("user2")

    # Cancel the order
    system.update_order_status(order_id, "cancelled")

    # Verify order cancellation
    state = system.get_system_state()
    assert state["orders"][order_id]["status"] == "cancelled"
    assert state["products"]["phone"]["available_stock"] == 5

def test_edge_case_delete_product_with_reservation():
    system = EShopSystem()

    # Create user and add product
    system.create_user("user3")
    system.add_or_update_product("tablet", 300.0, 3)

    # Add product to cart and perform checkout
    system.add_to_cart("user3", "tablet", 2)
    order_id = system.perform_checkout("user3")

    # Delete the product while it's reserved
    system.delete_product("tablet")

    # Verify product deletion and reservation handling
    state = system.get_system_state()
    assert "tablet" not in state["products"]
    assert state["orders"][order_id]["products"]["tablet"] == 2

===== OUTPUT_review_activity_diagram OUTPUT =====
Here are the identified defects in the activity diagram:

- Missing validation for product existence and stock availability before adding to cart (diagram shows direct addition without checks present in `add_to_cart` method)
- Missing atomicity handling in checkout process (diagram shows sequential steps without showing the try-catch rollback mechanism present in `perform_checkout`)
- Incorrect order status transition flow (diagram shows direct transition from pending to completed, skipping "processing" state which is required in the code)
- Missing "cancelled" status handling (diagram doesn't show the terminal "cancelled" state which is a key part of the status transition logic)
- Missing stock adjustment logic for order cancellations (diagram doesn't show the stock return process that occurs during status updates)
- Missing price snapshot storage (diagram shows "store price snapshot" but doesn't indicate it's used for recreating products during cancellations)
- Missing validation of reserved quantities matching cart quantities (diagram's checkout condition only checks cart emptiness and product existence, missing the reservation validation present in code)

:initialize e-shop system;
:create user "user1";
:create user "user2";
:add product "Laptop" with price and stock;

if (product exists and stock available) then (yes)
  :add 1 Laptop to user1's cart;
else (no)
  :show error "Product unavailable";
endif
if (product exists and stock available) then (yes)
  :add 2 Phones to user1's cart;
else (no)
  :show error "Product unavailable";
endif
if (cart not empty and products exist and reserved quantities match cart) then (yes)
  :calculate order total;
  :generate unique order ID;
  :create order with pending status;
  :store price snapshot for cancellation;
  :clear user's cart and reservations;
  :return order ID;
  repeat
    :update order status to processing;
    backward:rollback on failure;
    repeat while (status update successful?) is (no)
  ->yes;
  :update order status to completed;
  if (order cancelled?) then (yes)
    :update order status to cancelled;
    :return stock from price snapshot;
    stop
  endif
else (no)
  :abort checkout;
endif
:get system state;
stop
